These results indicate that uncertainty-aware dynamic representation and post-training recovery provide complementary mechanisms for improving learned local navigation.

</details>


### [138] [Spatial and Semantic Reasoning for LLM-Driven Robot Navigation via MCP](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27340)
*Jungsoo Lee,Jaegyun Park,Wansoo Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一个非侵入式的 LLM-ROS 导航框架：通过导航导向的表示层（占据栅格→度量位姿感知图像、路点级语义标注），并用 MCP 标准化为可复用工具，供任意 MCP 兼容 LLM 调用。仿真室内环境中，自主建图、空间推理导航、语义推理导航三类任务实现 >97% 地图覆盖率并可从自然语言指令选择导航目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 作为机器人自然语言接口已普及，但与 ROS 导航栈的集成受两点限制：(1) 占据栅格等导航数据是原始几何消息，难以直接作为 LLM 的空间/语义上下文；(2) 增加 LLM 能力通常需要自定义封装或机器人专用接口，跨系统复用性差。因此需要非侵入式、可复用的表示与接口方案。

Method: 非侵入式框架连接 LLM 推理与 ROS 导航：1) 导航导向表示层——视觉地图模块将占据栅格转为 metric、pose-aware 图像以支持目标推理；2) 语义标注模块以机器人位姿记录路点级观测；3) 通过 Model Context Protocol (MCP) 暴露为标准化、可复用工具，任意 MCP 兼容 LLM 无需机器人专用封装即可访问。不修改现有 ROS 导航栈。

Result: 在仿真室内环境中评估三类任务：自主建图、基于空间推理的导航、基于语义推理的导航。被测 LLM 后端利用该表示实现超过 97% 的地图覆盖率，并能从自然语言指令中选择空间或语义导航目标。

Conclusion: 表明在无需修改现有 ROS 导航栈的前提下，通过表示中介实现 LLM 导航是可行的，且 MCP 标准化提升了跨系统可复用性。局限在于验证仅在仿真室内环境，缺少真实机器人部署与更复杂场景的泛化证据。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used as natural-language interfaces for robotic systems, yet their integration with Robot Operating System (ROS)-based navigation remains limited by two gaps. First, navigation data such as occupancy grids are represented as raw geometric messages that are difficult for LLMs to use directly as spatial or semantic context. Second, adding LLM-driven capabilities often requires custom wrappers or robot-specific interfaces, limiting reuse across systems. To address these challenges, we propose a non-invasive framework that connects LLM reasoning with ROS-based navigation through a navigation-oriented representation layer, exposed through the Model Context Protocol (MCP) as standardized, reusable tools so that any MCP-compatible LLM can access them without robot-specific wrappers. The visual map modules transform occupancy grids into metric, pose-aware images for goal reasoning, while the semantic annotation modules record waypoint-level observations with robot poses. We evaluate the framework on three tasks: autonomous mapping, spatial reasoning-based navigation, and semantic reasoning-based navigation. The results show that the evaluated LLM backends use these representations to achieve over 97% map coverage and select spatial or semantic navigation targets from natural-language instructions in a simulated indoor environment. This demonstrates representation-mediated LLM navigation without modifying the existing ROS navigation stack.

</details>


### [139] [BladeMaster: Real-Time Robotic Cutting Simulation with Online-Generated Persistent Discontinuities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27342)
*Zhanyu Yang,Yunuo Chen,Yanjia Huang,Joseph Masterjohn,Yin Yang,Chenfanfu Jiang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: BladeMaster：基于全拉格朗日物质点法（TLMPM）的GPU加速切割仿真框架，用刀刃几何在线生成的持久侧标签编码切割历史，实现渐进/交叉切割、切面接触与双向工具-材料耦合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 切割同时改变可变形物体的形状与拓扑，现有仿真器难以在切割过程中跟踪刀具、在刀具撤出后保持不连续面，并让新暴露的表面彼此及与工具交互。既有方法常需预先给定切割面，或依赖辅助几何场耦合材料分离。

Method: 在全拉格朗日物质点法上构建GPU加速框架。核心思想是将切割历史直接编码在物质点上：依据刀刃几何在线生成持久的"侧标签"，由标签控制粒子-网格耦合——完好材料内部保持连通性，切面之间在工具撤出后禁止虚假耦合。无需预定义切割面或复制粒子即可支持渐进与交叉切割；引入材料-材料接触使切面可再接触与滑移而不重新连接，并采用双向工具-材料耦合传递反作用力。

Result: 在代表性任务上实现了工具驱动切割及随后的操作（manipulation），运行速度快于实时。

Conclusion: 以物质点上的持久侧标签统一处理切割拓扑变化、切面接触与工具耦合，为机器人操作中的可变形体切割提供无需预定义切割面、可实时运行的仿真方案。

Abstract: Cutting changes both the shape and topology of deformable objects, making accurate simulation challenging for robotic manipulation. A simulator must track the cutting tool as a cut develops, preserve the resulting discontinuities after tool withdrawal, and enable newly exposed surfaces to interact with the tool and with each other. Existing formulations often prescribe cut surfaces in advance or couple material separation to auxiliary geometric fields. We introduce BladeMaster, a GPU-accelerated cutting framework based on the total Lagrangian material point method (TLMPM). Our key idea is to encode the cutting history directly on material points through persistent side labels generated online from the blade geometry. These labels govern particle-grid coupling, preserving connectivity within intact material while preventing spurious coupling across cut faces after tool withdrawal. Our formulation supports progressive and intersecting cuts without predefined cut surfaces or particle duplication. Material-material contact enables cut surfaces to recontact and slide against each other without reconnecting, while two-way tool-material coupling allows material reaction forces to influence tool motion. Experiments demonstrate tool-driven cutting followed by manipulation, with faster-than-real-time performance on representative tasks.

</details>


### [140] [Reflection-Aware Reasoning for Non-Line-of-Sight Pedestrian Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27346)
*Byeonggyu Park,Mingu Jeon,Seong-Woo Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出反射感知框架，融合前视相机与2D雷达点云，在BEV空间推断反射阶数与反射面分布，并用物理引导光线追踪重建多径反射路径，实现ego车辆运动下的NLOS行人定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市自动驾驶中，对非视距（NLOS）行人的可靠定位对安全性至关重要；但在ego车辆自身运动的外场环境中，雷达多径传播复杂且噪声大，现有方法难以稳定定位被遮挡行人。

Method: 相机-雷达前融合：融合前视图像与2D雷达点云，在鸟瞰图（BEV）空间推断反射阶数与反射面分布；随后采用物理引导的光线追踪（ray tracing）重建扭曲的反射路径，反推并定位被遮挡的行人。

Result: 在ego动态条件下的外场测试床（outdoor testbed）场景中进行了验证，结果表明该框架对运动ego车辆下的NLOS行人定位有效。摘要未给出量化指标（如定位误差、召回率或对比基线）。

Conclusion: 该工作验证了结合反射几何推理与多模态感知在动态自车平台上进行NLOS行人定位的可行性，但其泛化性与定量性能仍需进一步评估。

Abstract: Reliable localization of non-line-of-sight (NLOS) pedestrians is critical for safe urban autonomous driving, yet it remains highly challenging in ego-dynamic outdoor environments, where ego-vehicle motion makes radar multipath propagation complex and noisy. In this paper, we present a reflection-aware framework for NLOS pedestrian localization with a moving ego-vehicle in outdoor testbed scenarios. Our framework fuses front-view camera images and 2D radar point clouds to infer reflection orders and reflective surface distributions in bird's-eye-view space. It then uses physics-guided ray tracing to reconstruct distorted reflection paths and localize the hidden pedestrian. We validate the framework in outdoor testbed scenarios under ego-dynamic conditions. The results demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed framework for NLOS pedestrian localization with a moving ego-vehicle.

</details>


### [141] [Omnidirectional Amphibious Locomotion via Internal Mass Actuation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27358)
*Niko Weaver,Boxi Xia,Li-Yu Lo,Yuhao Huang,Boyuan Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出全封闭球形两栖滚动机器人MARBLE，完全依靠内部三正交直线滑块的质量重分布驱动，同一刚性外壳在陆地产生滚动、在水中配合被动鳍实现水面推进，无需机构重构或额外驱动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 野外机器人需在多变地形与障碍中行进，同时耐受水、泥沙、植被和物理碰撞。现有足式/轮式平台机构外露、易受损，且跨介质（陆-水）移动通常需要更换机构或增加专用推进器，难以兼顾全向机动、密封性与跨介质能力。

Method: 三项正交直线滑块移动内部质量块，通过角动量/质心偏移产生机体旋转；方向感知控制器将平面速度指令映射为滑块位置。刚性球壳兼作陆地接触面、浮力外壳与被动鳍的安装结构，鳍在水中提供水面推进。球形形态使机器人在与地形、障碍接触时可自适应调整姿态与接触点，无需机械重构或多套驱动。

Result: 通过全向移动表征、异构陆地环境穿越、水面移动、陆-水过渡以及人为障碍交互等实验验证：单一封闭机构可同时实现全向移动、跨介质行进与对环境接触的容错性。

Conclusion: MARBLE以紧凑的全封闭机构实现异构地形、障碍与陆-水过渡的野外移动能力，软硬件将全部开源，为密封式跨介质机器人设计提供了可复用的方案。

Abstract: Field robots must traverse varied terrain and obstacles while remaining robust to water, debris, vegetation, and physical contact. We present MARBLE, a fully enclosed omnidirectional amphibious rolling robot driven entirely by internal mass redistribution. Three mutually orthogonal linear sliders shift internal masses to generate body rotation, while an orientation-aware controller maps planar velocity commands into slider positions. A rigid spherical shell encloses all active mechanisms and simultaneously serves as the terrestrial contact surface, buoyant enclosure, and mounting structure for passive fins that enable water-surface propulsion. Rotation of the same shell architecture hence produces rolling on land and surface propulsion in water without mechanical reconfiguration or separate locomotion actuators. The spherical morphology further allows the robot to accommodate changes in body orientation and contact location during direct interactions with terrain and obstacles. We evaluate MARBLE through omnidirectional locomotion characterization, traversal across heterogeneous terrestrial environments, aquatic surface locomotion, land-water transitions, and deliberate obstacle interactions. These experiments demonstrate how a single enclosed mechanical architecture can combine omnidirectional mobility, cross-medium locomotion, and tolerance to environmental contact. MARBLE provides a compact design for field mobility across heterogeneous terrain, obstacles, and land-water transitions. We will open-source all software and hardware design. Our website is this https URL

</details>


### [142] [From LiDAR Maps to Visual Localization: Unified Visual Association for Robust Point-Line-Plane Pose Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27363)
*Wentao Zhao,Zikun Chen,Yihe Niu,Haoyu Chen,Jingchuan Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出统一相机定位框架：将LiDAR地图的几何与反射率渲染为带2D-3D溯源信息的"准图像"，使相机图像与地图视图共享视觉特征与匹配器，实现全局定位与连续6-DoF位姿跟踪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相机在LiDAR先验地图中定位可提供长期几何参考，但图像与点云之间的模态鸿沟使跨模态匹配困难，通常需专门设计图像-LiDAR对应模型，泛化与鲁棒性受限。

Method: 将地图几何与反射率渲染为LiDAR导出的准图像，附带显式2D-3D溯源；借此在统一视觉接口上建立点、线对应，溯源信息用于恢复度量几何与线支撑平面约束；并提出分布感知、可观测性互补的优化策略，将候选关联分布传播为方向性位姿信息不确定性，按对弱位姿方向的互补能力选择性强化结构因子。

Result: 在EuRoC MAV基准与自采真实序列上实现准确全局定位与鲁棒连续6-DoF跟踪，仅依赖预建LiDAR地图作为持久先验，在剧烈光照变化与动态遮挡下仍保持稳定。

Conclusion: 以"可视化寻址"替代专用跨模态对应模型，为长期机器人导航提供可复用的视觉-度量定位范式，并提升弱几何与模糊关联下的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Camera localization in a prior LiDAR map provides a persistent geometric reference for long-term robotic navigation, yet remains challenging because of the substantial modality gap between camera images and point-cloud maps. We present a unified localization framework that makes the LiDAR map visually addressable rather than relying on a dedicated image-LiDAR correspondence model. Map geometry and reflectivity are rendered into LiDAR-derived quasi-images with explicit 2D-3D provenance, enabling camera observations and rendered map views to share mature visual features and matchers for both global localization and continuous pose tracking. Point and line correspondences are established through this common visual interface, while the retained provenance recovers metric LiDAR geometry and line-supported planar constraints for pose estimation. To improve robustness under ambiguous associations and weak geometry, we further introduce a distribution-aware, observability-complementary optimization strategy. Instead of reducing matching ambiguity to a scalar confidence, candidate association distributions are propagated into directional pose-information uncertainty, and reliable structural factors are selectively reinforced according to their ability to complement the currently weak pose directions. Experiments on the EuRoC MAV benchmark and self-collected real-world sequences demonstrate accurate global localization and robust continuous 6-DoF tracking using only a pre-built LiDAR map as the persistent prior, including under severe illumination variations and dynamic occlusions.

</details>


### [143] [CoPRE: Improving Sensitivity in Proprioceptive Contact Detection for Low-Cost Robot Arms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27381)
*Yuxiao Zhu,Jinzhou Li,Yifei Dong,Muhammad Suhail,Chunyuan Yang,Xinyuan Luo,Haoyu Li,Boyuan Chen,Xianyi Cheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CoPRE 仅用无接触运动数据学习关节力矩期望，通过残差加噪声加权雅可比映射为接触分数，在 ARX 与 Unitree G1 上显著提升无接触传感器条件下的弱接触检测召回率，并支撑基于信念空间的避障操作规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低成本机械臂通常无专用力/触觉传感器，本体感知中由弱接触引起的信号变化常被运动本身的噪声与正常波动淹没，接触检测困难；同时希望避免依赖力传感器、接触标签和解析动力学模型。

Method: 提出 CoPRE：仅利用无接触运动数据，从本体感知状态历史与指令运动估计无接触条件下的期望关节力矩，并剔除可能已包含接触的近期观测；将期望与观测力矩的残差通过噪声加权雅可比映射为接触分数。

Result: 在 ARX Arm 与 Unitree G1 真机实验中，测试接触试验召回率达 74.1% 与 82.2%，而学习力矩预测/逆动力学基线为 ARX 0%/0%、G1 16.3%/42.2%；对 3.5 N（ARX）和 5.5 N（G1）推力的检测率达 90%；并实现信念空间操作规划，用于避障物体放置与插书任务，接触更新空间信念使机器人能从受阻运动退避、调整位姿并重试。

Conclusion: CoPRE 证明无需力/触觉传感器与接触标签，仅凭无接触运动的自监督残差建模即可实现灵敏的本体感知接触检测，并将检测到的接触用于下游规划与重试行为，具备低成本机器人部署价值。

Abstract: Contact detection during robotic manipulation allows robots to recognize unexpected contact and adapt their motion accordingly. However, in low-cost robot arms without dedicated force or tactile sensors, detecting weak contacts from proprioception is challenging because the resulting changes in joint-level proprioceptive signals can be small compared to normal variation and noise caused by robot motion itself. We introduce Contact-free Proprioceptive Response Estimation (CoPRE), improving proprioceptive contact detection sensitivity using only contact-free motion, without additional force sensors, contact labels, or analytical dynamics models. CoPRE estimate the expected joint torques under contact-free motion from proprioceptive state history and commanded motion, while removing recent observations that may already reflect contact. It then computes the residual between the expected and observed joint torque estimates, and maps this residual to a contact score using a noise-weighted Jacobian. Real-robot experiments on ARX Arm and Unitree G1 show that CoPRE achieves 74.1% and 82.2% recall on the tested contact trials, compared with 0%/0% on ARX and 16.3%/42.2% on G1 for the learned torque-prediction and inverse-dynamics baselines. CoPRE also reaches 90% detection rate for pushing force at 3.5 N on ARX and 5.5 N on G1. To demonstrate the downstream utility of our method, we implement belief-space manipulation planning for obstacle-aware object placement and book insertion where detected contacts update the spatial belief and enable the robot to retreat from blocked motions, adjust its pose, and retry. Project website at this https URL

</details>


### [144] [Automotive mmWave Spinning Radar Place Recognition with Spatially Gated Feature-Correlation Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27394)
*Saimunur Rahman,Sagun Singh Shrestha,Abdelwahed Khamis,Peyman Moghadam*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SGCA-Net：将旋转鲁棒特征提取与空间门控相关聚合结合，用于旋转FMCW雷达的场所识别，在MulRan上超越SOTA且对航向变化鲁棒，并零微调泛化到HeRCULES。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 车载旋转FMCW雷达具备360°稠密感知且不受光照与天气影响，适合自动驾驶导航中的场所识别与重定位。但航向变化在极坐标表示中表现为循环移位，常规全局聚合会丢失对区分相似地点重要的雷达响应间关系。

Method: 提出SGCA-Net框架：旋转鲁棒特征提取 + 空间门控相关聚合（SGCA）。SGCA学习空间权重以抑制不稳定、歧义雷达区域的影响，同时聚合局部响应之间的成对相关性，保留有判别力的特征关系。

Result: 在MulRan数据集的城市、校园、开放道路环境中持续优于SOTA，对大幅航向变化保持鲁棒；在HeRCULES数据集上无需微调即可泛化到未见环境与不同雷达传感器。

Conclusion: SGCA-Net通过旋转不变的相关聚合提升了旋转雷达场所识别的精度与泛化性，为长期自主导航中的重定位提供了有效的雷达方案。

Abstract: Automotive spinning FMCW radar provides dense, $360^\circ$ sensing and remains reliable under poor illumination and adverse weather, making it well-suited to autonomous navigation. Place recognition uses these observations to identify previously visited locations for re-localization and long-term navigation. However, heading changes appear as circular shifts in the polar radar representation, and conventional global aggregation can lose relationships among radar responses that are important for distinguishing similar places. We propose SGCA-Net, a spinning radar place recognition framework that combines rotation-robust feature extraction with Spatially Gated Correlation Aggregation (SGCA). SGCA learns spatial weights to reduce the influence of unstable and ambiguous radar regions, while aggregating pairwise correlations among local responses to preserve informative feature relationships. Experiments on the MulRan dataset show that SGCA-Net consistently outperforms SOTA methods across urban, campus, and open-road environments, while remaining robust to substantial heading variation. Evaluation on the HeRCULES dataset further demonstrates that SGCA-Net generalizes to unseen environments and radar sensors without fine-tuning.

</details>


### [145] [Compressed delayed-information projection for six-degree-of-freedom underwater vehicle navigation under delayed acoustic positioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27439)
*Shuyue Li,Miguel López-Benítez,Eng Gee Lim,Fei Ma,Qian Dong,Mengze Cao,Limin Yu,Xiaohui Qin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出压缩延迟信息投影（CDIP），用于UUV延迟声学导航的因果15维误差状态卡尔曼滤波，避免全量回放即可把延迟观测的修正投影到当前状态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 延迟声学定位包约束的是历史导航状态，当前时刻更新会与错配状态比较，而精确回放需重执行中间估计器历史，计算代价高。

Method: 保留源历元快照及历史到当前互协方差，将延迟声学修正直接投影到当前状态，构成因果15状态ESKF；以精确固定滞后回放/重演的OOSM处理作为高保真参考。

Result: 154组配对记录（1.5 s固定声学延迟、无中断）：轨迹位置RMSE均值由基线1.062 m降至0.456 m（57.1%）；0.456 m较回放均值0.451 m高1.03%，单次更新运行时间均值降低99.2%（约127倍）。六种固定延迟扫描各30组配对记录及9维一致性分析；50–300 s声学中断下保持接近回放的精度与亚毫秒更新耗时。

Conclusion: CDIP在受评估配置下提供了精度—计算权衡的紧凑延迟信息处理；证据未确立与回放的统计等价性或非劣性。

Abstract: Delayed acoustic positioning packets constrain historical navigation states, but a current-time update evaluates them against a mismatched state, whereas exact rewind/replay re-executes the intervening estimator history. This paper introduces compressed delayed-information projection (CDIP), a causal 15-state error-state Kalman filter (ESKF) treatment for delayed-acoustic unmanned underwater vehicle (UUV) navigation. CDIP retains a source-epoch snapshot and the historical-to-current cross-covariance, then projects the delayed source-epoch acoustic correction directly to the current state without full rewind/replay. Exact fixed-lag rewind/replay out-of-sequence-measurement (OOSM) processing serves as a high-fidelity accuracy reference. In 154 usable paired recordings at a fixed 1.5-s acoustic delay without an outage, CDIP reduced mean trajectory-position root-mean-square error (RMSE) from 1.062 m for the baseline to 0.456 m (57.1%). Its 0.456-m mean was 1.03% higher than the 0.451-m replay mean, while its measured mean per-update runtime was 99.2% lower (approximately 127-fold). A separate predeclared sweep across six fixed delays, with 30 paired recordings per delay, and a truth-supported 9-D consistency analysis bound the interpretation. Additional targeted experiments showed near-replay trajectory accuracy across 50-300-s acoustic outages while preserving sub-millisecond update cost. CDIP therefore provides a compact delayed-information treatment with an empirical accuracy-computation trade-off under the evaluated configuration; the evidence does not establish statistical equivalence or non-inferiority relative to replay.

</details>


### [146] [X2Real: an eXtensive simulation benchmark for real-world generalist policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27449)
*Lian Ruan,Jade Yang,Sherphylan Gao,Felix Gao,Kyson Liang,Galen Liu,Ligo Wu,Lane Jin,Guu Gu,Bevan Xie,Cloud Yan,Zongzi Yuan,Kino Luo,Emma Chen,Shuwen Chen,Yang Ping,Miles Guo,Rain Sun,Kayden Zhang,Alex Du,Ruihai Wu,Liang Hao,Zhaoshuo Li,Roy Gan,Hao Wang,Qian Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: X2Real：基于 Isaac Lab-Arena 的可进化仿真基准，通过视觉与物理标定实现 sim-real 0.84 线性相关，含 10 能力维度、44 个长时序任务，并用多轴域随机化与严格隔离的训练-评测流程保障公平性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用机器人操作策略发展迅速，但现有仿真基准存在明显 sim-to-real 差距、任务覆盖窄、训练-测试流程模糊导致评测不公平等问题；已有工作仅部分解决，且静态基准设计难以支撑策略的长期迭代发展。作者希望构建同时满足保真性、多样性与公平性的可进化评测基础设施。

Method: 基于 Nvidia Isaac Lab-Arena 构建仿真平台；以保真性、多样性、公平性三原则为核心，对仿真的视觉与物理属性进行标定以对齐真实硬件；建立含 10 个能力维度、44 个分层长时序任务的分类体系（涵盖基础操作技能以及视觉定位、语言理解、双臂控制等高级能力）；采用多轴域随机化与严格不相交的训练-评测流程防止基准被利用；通过自定义物理领域专用语言支撑 Mana 仿真生态，实现模块化任务设计与迭代式性能分析，并附带近 300 小时标注仿真轨迹数据集。

Result: 仿真与真机评测结果之间达到 0.84 的线性相关，表明仿真评测在较高程度上可反映真实性能；构建了覆盖 10 个能力维度、44 个任务的层级化任务体系与约 300 小时标注轨迹数据集，形成模块化、可迭代的评测与任务设计生态。

Conclusion: X2Real 提供了一个保真、多样且公平的可进化评测基础设施，在有效性上弥合 sim-to-real 评测鸿沟，为通用机器人操作策略的持续发展提供支撑。不过其核心贡献仍是评测基准与仿真基础设施，而非新的策略学习方法，实际价值取决于社区采纳与后续真机验证。

Abstract: Generalist robot manipulation policies have developed rapidly, yet their reliable evaluation remains challenging due to fundamental flaws in existing simulation benchmarks: prominent sim-to-real gaps, narrow task coverage, and unfair evaluation caused by ambiguous training-test pipelines. Prior works only partially resolve these issues and lack simultaneous faithfulness, diversity, and fairness, while static benchmark designs fail to sustain long-term policy development. We present X2Real, an evolvable simulation benchmark for faithfully evaluating the real-world performance of robotic manipulation policies based on Nvidia Isaac Lab-Arena. Following three core principles (faithfulness, diversity, and fairness), X2Real calibrates simulation visual and physical properties to align with real hardware, achieving a 0.84 linear correlation between simulated and real-robot evaluation results. It features a comprehensive taxonomy with 10 capability dimensions and 44 hierarchical long-horizon tasks, covering basic manipulation skills and advanced capacities such as visual grounding, language understanding, and bimanual control. We further adopt multi-axis domain randomization and strictly disjoint training-evaluation pipelines to mitigate benchmark exploitation and ensure credible evaluation. Powered by a custom physical domain-specific language, the Mana simulation ecosystem supports modular task design and iterative performance analysis, alongside a nearly 300-hour annotated simulation trajectory dataset. X2Real offers a faithful, diverse, and fair evolving evaluation infrastructure, effectively bridging the sim-to-real evaluation gap and supporting the advancement of generalist robotic manipulation policies.

</details>


### [147] [BEE: Intervention-Adaptive Real-World Reinforcement Learning with Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27450)
*Weihui Zhao,Xiaohan Yan,Zunian Wan,Xuan Du,Zhaozhan Chi,Jianbo Mao,Ruipu Wu,Rushuai Yang,Houlin Li,Shukai Yang,Jing Wu,Yuxiang Yan,Yongcheng Liu,Chuankang Li,Guanghui Ren,Wei Shan,Maoqing Yao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: BEE: 在冻结VLA上进行真实世界在线RL的干预自适应框架，把人类纠正视为对约束的证据（按动作维度预测纠正一致性以设定约束松紧），在3个真实操作任务+1个LIBERO-Pro任务上平均成功率91.2%（RLT 57.5%、DSRL 42.1%），且人类干预率最低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程操作的成功往往取决于少数精度关键阶段，毫米级误差会抹掉先前全部进展；在线RL可精确优化这些动作，但真机上自由探索代价过高，必须依赖人类纠正。然而现有面向VLA的在线RL方法要么无法纳入人类纠正，要么将其当作无差别监督信号对待。作者观察到人类纠正并非均匀噪声：在某些动作维度上是可靠的，在另一些维度上则多变。基于此，本文希望让策略"超越专家模仿"（BEyond Expert imitation）。

Method: 在冻结的VLA策略之上构建干预自适应在线RL框架BEE。不改写VLA权重，而是将人类纠正重新定义为关于约束的证据而非待复现的动作：训练一个Correction Model，输入给定VLA提议，预测人类将如何纠正该提议，以及该纠正在每个动作维度上的一致程度。预测出的一致性用于设定策略优化中按动作维度划分的约束紧度——纠正一致处策略紧贴人类，纠正多变处约束放松。

Result: 在3个真实世界操作任务与1个LIBERO-Pro仿真任务上、以匹配的在线数据预算评测：BEE在每个任务上都取得最高成功率，平均91.2%，对比RLT 57.5%、DSRL 42.1%；在所有真实世界任务上人类干预率最低。

Conclusion: 将人类干预按动作维度视为一致性可变的约束证据，而非统一监督信号，能有效提升冻结VLA的真实世界在线RL效率与精度，同时显著降低人类干预需求。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models handle long-horizon manipulation, yet success hinges on a few precision-critical phases where millimeter-scale errors undo all prior progress. Online reinforcement learning (RL) can optimize exactly these actions, but free exploration is far too costly on real robots, which makes human corrections indispensable. However, existing online RL methods for VLAs either cannot incorporate such corrections or fold them into undifferentiated supervision. Yet human corrections are not uniformly noisy but reliable along some action dimensions and variable along others. Building on this, we introduce BEE, an intervention-adaptive framework for real-world RL on a frozen VLA that lets the policy go BEyond Expert imitation. We formulate human corrections not as actions to reproduce but as evidence about a constraint: a Correction Model predicts how a human would correct a given VLA proposal and how consistent the correction is along each action dimension. This predicted consistency sets the per-dimension tightness of a constraint on policy optimization. Where corrections are consistent the policy stays close to the human, and where they vary, the constraint relaxes. We evaluate BEE on three real-world manipulation tasks and one LIBERO-Pro simulation task at a matched online-data budget. BEE attains the highest success rate on every task, 91.2% on average against 57.5% for RLT and 42.1% for DSRL, and the lowest human intervention rate on all real-world tasks.

</details>


### [148] [Safety-Filtered Distributed Koopman-MPC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27463)
*Shengjun Zhang,Wenhao Li,Zhenxin Lin,Zhenglong Sun*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 将DMPC中"预测"与"碰撞约束"解耦：邻居轨迹仅驱动Koopman-MPC，安全性由本地传感与货架几何构成的硬约束QP投影保证，给出采样间分离定理、max-min可行性判据与传感半径条件，并在8机器人仓库仿真中验证零碰撞与全目标达成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DMPC通常同时用邻居轨迹构造预测与碰撞约束，丢包会同时破坏二者。作者希望让安全约束不依赖通信，仅依赖本地传感，从而在丢包下仍保持硬安全保证；同时保留邻居轨迹用于性能预测。

Method: 分离式架构：接收轨迹驱动Koopman-MPC做性能预测；局部传感+货架几何定义硬约束QP，投影实际输入。其径向需求为在单个零阶保持区间内使支撑平面间隙非负的最小恒定加速度；互补对行在不交换安全决策下恢复耦合需求。理论含采样间分离定理（有界快照与方向性模型误差）、同时局部可行性的精确max-min检验、切换交互图的传感半径条件；高阶预测行可松弛，有限保持行无安全松弛。

Result: 匹配的8机器人仓库仿真（冻结Koopman模型、非线性漂移、有界输入与速度、货架约束、120ms周期、丢包）：完整控制器20/20试验零碰撞且160/160到达目标；无最终投影的Koopman-MPC仅1/20零碰撞。38,400组硬约束行集全部通过在线可行性检验，局部QP均求解成功。五流集群扫描至16机器人都零碰撞且硬行可行，20机器人边界仅在线裕度转负后失败，重建的每智能体关键路径低于采样周期。有界传感与差速驱动测试提供额外应力验证。

Conclusion: 通过将安全约束从通信依赖的邻居预测中剥离、交由本地传感几何驱动的硬QP投影，可在丢包与有界误差下获得采样间硬安全与可行性保证，代价是不可松弛的有限保持行，性能预测仍可由Koopman-MPC承担。

Abstract: Distributed model predictive control (DMPC) often constructs both predictions and collision constraints from neighbor trajectories, so packet loss can remove both. We separate these roles: received trajectories drive Koopman-MPC, while local sensing and shelf geometry define a hard-constrained quadratic program (QP) that projects the applied input. Its radial demand is the least constant acceleration that keeps a supporting-plane clearance nonnegative throughout one zero-order-hold interval. Complementary pair rows recover the coupled demand without exchanging safety decisions. We give an intersample separation theorem under bounded snapshot and directional plant errors, an exact max-min test for simultaneous local feasibility, and a sensing-radius condition for switching interaction graphs. Anticipatory high-order rows may be relaxed for performance, but the finite-hold rows contain no safety slack. Matched eight-robot warehouse simulations use a frozen Koopman model, nonlinear drift, bounded inputs and speed, shelf constraints, a 120 ms control period, and packet dropout. The full controller is collision-free in 20/20 matched trials and reaches 160/160 robot goals; predictive Koopman-MPC without the final projection is collision-free in 1/20 trials. All 38,400 full-method hard-row sets pass the online feasibility test, and every local QP solves. Five-stream fleet sweeps are collision-free and hard-row feasible through 16 robots; the 20-robot boundary fails only after the online margin turns negative, while the reconstructed per-agent critical path remains below the sampling period. Bounded-sensing and differential-drive tests provide additional deployment stress.

</details>


### [149] [A Modular Dual-Arm Robotic Cell for Disassembly and Repair of Industrial Control Electronics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27466)
*Maximilian Ruhe,Fabian Harlacher,Christian Friedrich,Martin Kipfmueller*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一个面向工业控制电子设备（PLC、伺服驱动器、操作面板）维修拆解的模块化双臂机器人工作站，基于ROS 2与行为树集成序列知识、视觉/力感知与力受限技能，在仿真、遥操作与真机上统一验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业控制电子设备在工厂维护中常被人工返修，但其结构并非为自动拆解而设计，缺少面向维修的机器人系统架构。作者希望将拆解序列知识、感知、验证与力感知技能统一到一个可复用的机器人平台中。

Method: 硬件为两台协作机械臂，配可更换末端工具、RGB-D与腕部视觉、力/力矩传感；软件为ROS 2控制栈，采用行为树执行，支持遥操作、数字孪生与有界学习型接触技能。流程分解为序列规划、带回退的符号执行、力受限工具技能、视觉状态评估与基于示教的适应。核心表示是由CAD导出的设备图，编码拆解顺序、可达/接近约束、所需工具、可行拆卸方向与验证状态，并转换为行为树与运动层的操作对象。

Result: 在三种代表性设备上验证，覆盖螺钉拆除、损坏紧固件回退处理、卡扣开启、连接器释放、协作式PCB提取以及基于状态判定的维修决策，并在仿真、遥操作与真实硬件上贯通执行。

Conclusion: 主要贡献是一个统一架构，通过单一ROS 2接口把序列知识、感知、验证与力感知技能贯通于仿真、遥操作和真实硬件；定位为面向维修拆解的工程化系统集成，尚非通用拆解泛化方法。

Abstract: Industrial control electronics such as programmable logic controllers, servo drives and operator panels are routinely repaired in plant maintenance, but were never designed for automated disassembly. This paper presents a modular dual-arm robotic cell for repair-oriented disassembly, using two collaborative manipulators, interchangeable tools, red-green-blue-depth (RGB-D) and wristlevel perception, force/torque sensing and a Robot Operating System (ROS) 2- based control with Behavior Tree (BT) execution, teleoperation, digital-twin support and bounded learning-based contact skills. The process is decomposed into sequence planning, symbolic execution with fallbacks, force-limited tool skills, visual condition assessment and demonstration-based adaptation. A CADderived device graph encodes the disassembly order, access constraints, tools, feasible removal directions and verification states and converts them into operation objects for the BT and motion layers. Grounded in three representative devices, the cell covers screw removal, damaged-fastener fallback, snap-fit opening, connector release, cooperative printed circuit board (PCB) extraction and condition-based repair decisions. The main contribution is an architecture linking sequence knowledge, perception, verification and force-aware skills through one ROS 2 interface across simulation, teleoperation and real hardware.

</details>


### [150] [Kairos: Grounded Forecasting of Presence and Directional Flow in 4D Scene Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27467)
*Iacopo Catalano,Julio A. Placed,Javier Civera,Jorge Peña Queralta*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Kairos 将分层 3D 场景图扩展为 4D 场景图，每个体素存储方向混合分布与出现率，用谱预测器预测任意未来时刻的行人存在概率与完整方向分布，并在真实环境中验证其在规划任务上的优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期自主机器人在人类环境中运行时，需要预判未观测时段行人的移动方式。现有行人运动表示存在两难：要么只预测标量占据率而丢失方向信息，要么建模完整方向分布但固定于某一时刻，无法随时间查询。作者希望得到一种可对未来任意时刻同时给出存在概率与方向分布的可查询表示。

Method: 在分层 3D 场景图基础上构建 4D 场景图（4DSG）。对重建几何的每个被观测体素存储方向混合（directional mixture）与出现率（presence rate）；用谱预测器对任意未来查询时间进行预测。引入相邻体素间的成对流依赖以支持条件查询；每个体素给出预测方差，从而得到随观测积累而收紧的可信区间。表示在回环闭合校正下保持一致。

Result: 在三个真实行人环境评估：移动机器人采集的校园数据集、购物中心、连续录像十一个月的车站大厅。学习到的状态在回环闭合校正下保持一致性；预测能力与用完整检测流训练的专用占据/流模型相当，而 Kairos 仅利用巡逻机器人可获得的少量数据。在下游遭遇概率规划任务中，同等成功率下基于 Kairos 预测的路径比任何时不变地图遇到更多行人。

Conclusion: Kairos 提供了一种随时间可查询、带校准不确定性的方向流记忆表示，能在数据受限条件下支撑行人遭遇概率规划，适用于长期人类环境自主导航。

Abstract: Long-term autonomy in human-populated environments requires anticipating whether and how people will move at times a robot has not yet observed. Existing representations of pedestrian motion face a tradeoff: they either forecast future activity, reducing each location to a scalar rate, or model the full directional distribution, holding it fixed in time. We present Kairos, a predictive directional-flow memory that extends a hierarchical 3D scene graph (3DSG) to a 4D scene graph (4DSG). Every observed voxel of the reconstructed geometry stores a directional mixture and a presence rate, and spectral predictors forecast, for any future query time, both the probability that people are present and the full directional distribution of their motion. Pairwise flow dependence between adjacent voxels supports conditional queries, and per-voxel predictive variances yield calibrated credible intervals that tighten as observations accumulate. We evaluate Kairos on three real pedestrian environments: a robot-collected campus dataset, a shopping mall, and a station concourse recorded continuously for eleven months. Its learned state remains consistent under loop-closure corrections, and its forecasts are competitive with dedicated occupancy and flow models trained on the full detection stream, although Kairos learns from only the small fraction available to a patrolling robot. Finally, we validate the representation on a downstream encounter-probability planning task, where plans computed over the Kairos forecasts encounter more people than plans computed over any time-invariant map at an equal success rate. We provide the code at this https URL .

</details>


### [151] [Collocated Shape Regulation for Soft Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27469)
*Pietro Pustina,Ebrahim Shahabi,Daniel Feliu-Talegon,Alessandro De Luca,Cosimo Della Santina*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出首个连续体软体机器人协同定位（collocated）控制的通用框架，只调节驱动坐标即可保证其收敛，并在显式相容条件下保证整机形状收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续体软体机器人形状控制通常依赖精确动态模型并对所有自由度进行驱动，代价高且模型获取困难；而协同定位控制虽在刚性机器人中成熟，但因软体动力学复杂，难以推广。

Method: 建立连续体软体机器人协同定位控制的统一框架，推导PD、PID、PsatID及带补偿/抵消项的控制器族，并给出局部、半全局、全局三类稳定性保证与严格收敛性分析。

Result: 在不同模型离散化与控制器参数下进行了大量实验验证，证明所提方法有效；框架统一了已有方法并引入新控制器设计，且无需机器人完整动态模型。

Conclusion: 该框架为连续体软体机器人控制器选择与实现提供具已知稳定性保证的实用设计指南，显著降低对完整动态模型的依赖。

Abstract: Controlling the shape of a continuum soft robot typically requires an accurate dynamic model and actuation of all degrees of freedom. We show that regulating only the actuated coordinates, through collocated shape control, achieves provably stable convergence of those coordinates and, under an explicit compatibility condition, of the entire robot shape. While collocated control is a cornerstone of high-performance motion control in rigid robotics, extending this formulation to continuum soft robots has remained challenging due to the complexity of their dynamics. We present the first general framework for collocated control of continuum soft robots and derive a unified family of controllers, including PD, PID, PsatID, and their counterparts with compensation and cancellation components. The framework unifies existing approaches while introducing new controller designs. In particular, we develop three classes of PD and PID like regulators with local, semi-global, and global stability guarantees, and provide rigorous convergence analyses for each. Extensive experimental validation demonstrates the effectiveness of the proposed methods across different model discretizations and controller parameters. The resulting framework provides practical design guidelines for selecting and implementing controllers with known stability guarantees, without requiring a complete dynamic model of the robot

</details>


### [152] [RoboCafé in the Open: Interaction Continuity in Long-Term Public Human-Robot Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27475)
*Kaitlynn Taylor Pineda,Kush Kumar Kushwaha,Jie Wang,Jiaming Du,Anvii Mishra,Emilie Basu Suri,Angela Guo,Chien-Ming Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文开发了 RoboCafé——一台自主对话式咖啡机器人，具备任务感知对话、实时多模态感知与先前交互记忆能力，并在大学建筑内部署 12 天、处理 148 份订单，用以研究长期公共场景下人机交互的"交互连续性"，并据此提出四项系统设计需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期驻留公共空间的机器人必须应对人员、相遇与情境不断变化的情况，需要保持交互连续性。但以往研究缺少真实公共环境中长时间、重复交互的部署证据，系统设计层面的需求也不清晰。作者希望理解长期公共 HRI 中连续性为何失效、以及系统应具备哪些能力。

Method: 构建 RoboCafé 自主对话式咖啡机器人（任务感知对话 + 实时多模态感知 + 历史遭遇记忆），在校园建筑中进行 12 天现场部署，共接收 148 份订单，涵盖回头客、路人、团体变化与连续点单等场景，并对系统以订单为中心的交互模型假设被反复突破的情形进行观察与归纳。

Result: 部署中出现大量突破系统订单中心模型假设的情形；作者发现实现交互连续性的关键判断包括：当前在场者是谁、先前上下文归属何人、交互始于何处止于何处、以及系统对交互的表征是否与物理世界一致。由此提炼出四项系统设计需求：上下文交互状态、持久人物锚定、显式交互生命周期管理与交互可观测性。

Conclusion: 长期公共人机交互中的连续性不是单一记忆或感知模块能解决的问题，而需要状态表示、人物锚定、生命周期管理与可观测性协同设计的系统级方案；本文的需求清单可作为后续长期 HRI 系统的设计指导。

Abstract: As robots remain in public spaces over extended periods, they must maintain interaction continuity by preserving and correctly applying context as people, encounters, and circumstances change. To study interaction continuity in long-term public human-robot interactions, we developed RoboCafé, an autonomous conversational coffee robot designed to support repeated interactions through task-aware dialogue, real-time multimodal perception, and memory of prior encounters. We deployed RoboCafé for 12 days in a university building, where it received 148 orders. The deployment involved repeat customers, passersby, changing groups, and back-to-back orders that repeatedly crossed the boundaries assumed by the system's order-centered interaction model. We found that successful interaction continuity requires a robot to determine who is currently present, which prior context belongs to whom, where interactions begin and end, and whether its representation of an interaction matches what is occurring in the physical world. From these observations, we derive four system design requirements for maintaining interaction continuity in longitudinal public human-robot interactions: contextual interaction state, persistent person grounding, explicit interaction life-cycle management, and interaction observability.

</details>


### [153] [Behavior-Aligned Action Tokenization for Robot Policy Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27513)
*Junbo Dong,Ze Chen,Zhendong Xie,Junjie Li,Lixin Xu,Xuemin Chi,Yiming Song,Zhaoyuan Ma*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 BAAT（行为对齐动作分词），用 Soft-DTW 选取跨演示中时序不同的对应动作片段，并将量化坐标与重建联合对齐，使相似运动共享邻近的离散码本表示；配合历史条件扩散解码器与下游自回归策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自回归机器人策略的动作分词器主要靠重构目标训练，任务间共享的局部运动（时序不同但模式相似）缺乏显式行为对应监督，导致相似运动无法获得共享表示。

Method: 1) 用 Soft-DTW 在训练演示间软对齐并选取对应动作块；2) 对量化坐标施加对齐损失，与重建损失联合优化；3) 历史条件扩散解码器从 token 重建连续动作块；4) 下游自回归策略预测这些 token。消融为 LIBERO-All 对齐项开关。

Result: 三个仿真 benchmark 子任务与两个真机任务上，仿真平均成功率约 45.2%，较 OAT 高约 7.2 个百分点；LIBERO-All 对齐消融中策略成功率由 70.2% 提升至 79.0%，而轨迹回放成功率下降（说明表示更利于策略学习而非纯重建）。

Conclusion: 行为对应可作为动作分词器的有效监督信号，用于组织共享运动结构和提升下游策略学习，但代价是重建/回放保真度有所牺牲。

Abstract: Autoregressive robot policies learn continuous control by predicting discrete action tokens from observations. Different tasks often share local motions, yet behavioral correspondence across demonstrations receives limited explicit supervision in existing tokenizers. Motions with different timing can therefore lack a shared representation despite following similar patterns. We propose Behavior-Aligned Action Tokenization (BAAT), which uses soft dynamic time warping (Soft-DTW) to select corresponding action chunks and aligns their quantized coordinates jointly with reconstruction. This objective encourages similar motions across tasks to occupy nearby quantized representations while retaining executable action detail. A history-conditioned diffusion decoder reconstructs continuous action chunks from these tokens, and a downstream autoregressive policy learns to predict them. We evaluate BAAT on selected tasks from three simulation benchmarks and two real robot tasks. BAAT achieves a mean simulation success rate of approximately 45.2%, exceeding OAT by approximately 7.2 percentage points. In the controlled LIBERO-All alignment ablation, policy success rises from 70.2% to 79.0% while trajectory replay success decreases. These results support behavioral correspondence as supervision for organizing shared motion structure in action tokenizers and improving downstream robot policy learning.

</details>


### [154] [NavProbe: Evidence-Grounded Reasoning with Active Memory Retrieval for Zero-Shot Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27526)
*Jingyang Liu,Sujia Yao,Jiayuan Gu,Lan Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: NavProbe：分层零样本导航智能体，用紧凑索引+主动证据检索动态维护子目标议程，在R2R-CE/RxR-CE与ObjectNav上均超零样本基线，并有真机演示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程导航中，完整视觉历史处理代价高，而压缩摘要又可能丢失重新审视早期决策所需的细节；需要在“成本”与“可回溯性”之间取得平衡。

Method: 分层零样本智能体：紧凑索引将访问地点的摘要、转移与地标链接到视觉/几何记录；任务执行器在当前上下文不足时主动检索定向证据，生成、修订或消解子目标；可复用结论回写更新索引；技能策略把修订后的任务状态转成参数化导航动作。

Result: R2R-CE 71.7% SR / 55.8% SPL；RxR-CE 55.3% SR / 38.6% SPL，优于强零样本基线；HM3D-v2 ObjectNav 79.3% SR；并给出定性真机部署演示。

Conclusion: 通过“索引+主动检索+子目标修订”的闭环，无需训练即显著提升零样本长时程导航性能，并具备实机迁移潜力。

Abstract: Long-horizon navigation requires an agent to revise its intermediate objectives as evidence accumulates. Full visual histories are costly to process, while compact summaries may omit details needed to reconsider earlier decisions. We introduce NavProbe, a hierarchical zero-shot navigation agent that couples a dynamic subgoal agenda with active evidence retrieval. A compact index links summaries of visited places, transitions, and landmarks to their visual and geometric records. When the current context is insufficient, a task executive retrieves targeted evidence to generate, revise, or resolve subgoals. Reusable conclusions are used to update the index, and a skill policy converts the revised task state into parameterized navigation actions. NavProbe achieves 71.7% SR and 55.8% SPL on R2R-CE and 55.3% SR and 38.6% SPL on RxR-CE, outperforming strong zero-shot baselines. It also achieves 79.3% SR on HM3D-v2 ObjectNav, with qualitative real-robot demonstrations illustrating physical deployment.

</details>


### [155] [Behaviora - A Conceptual Architecture for External and Internal Behavior of Robots and Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27536)
*Gote Nyman*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Behaviora概念架构，用可寻址标识符（IoB Address）表示机器人与智能体的内外行为：Behavior Episode定义"做什么"，Style Profile定义"怎么做"，Experience Profile表示调制执行的内部状态，Behavior Compiler将行为表示编译为平台特定动作；以原始触碰臂模型演示组件关系


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体/机器人行为表示缺乏统一、可寻址的形式化手段，行为内容与表达风格（如何执行）常混杂，且外部动作与内部状态难以用同一原则刻画，导致行为难以跨平台复用、检索与编译

Method: 概念性架构设计。行为分解为Behavior Episode（由来自行为分类体系BTax的episode components构成），每个组件分配持久标识符即IoB Address；Style Profile编码行为表达风格（能力、文化礼仪等）；Experience Profile编码调制执行的内部状态；Behavior Compiler将上述表示映射到平台特定动作。内部行为采用同一episode原则并可渲染为inner speech。以primitive touching arm模型验证组件关系

Result: 以原始触碰臂模型展示了各组件及其相互关系，说明外部行为可由可寻址运动及其风格生成，内部行为可经同一表达原则渲染为内部言语；感知、感知处理与复杂任务上下文未纳入当前实现，仅预留概念位置

Conclusion: Behaviora提供了行为表示的概念框架与寻址机制，但当前实现停留在概念与玩具模型层面，未验证真实平台、感知闭环或复杂任务，尚未构成可落地的物理AI系统方法

Abstract: Behaviora is a preliminary conceptual architecture for representing agent and robot behavior, external and internal alike, in an addressable form. A behaving robot or agent performs a Behavior Episode composed of episode components, which can be derived from behavior taxonomies (BTax) and assigned persistent identifiers. We denote these identifiers as IoB (Internet of Behaviors) Addresses. A Behavior Episode specifies what the system does, while a Style Profile (SP) specifies how this behavior is expressed. Style can communicate characteristics of the actor and qualities such as competence and cultural manners. An Experience Profile (EP) represents behaviorally relevant internal state that modulates the execution of an Episode. Finally, a Behavior Compiler maps these behavioral representations to platform-specific actions. We use a primitive touching arm model to show these components and their relations. External Behavior is a result of addressable movements and their styles. Internal Behavior is represented through the same episodic principle and can be rendered as inner speech. Sensing, perception and complex task contexts have not been included in the present implementation, although a conceptual place is reserved for them.

</details>


### [156] [Gray-Box Model Predictive Control for Articulated Dump Trucks via Gaussian Process Learning of Sideslip](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27597)
*Arash Shahirpour,Jens Ahlers,Christopher Schulte,Tim Reuscher*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出用高斯过程回归（GPR）学习铰接式自卸车（ADT）的侧偏角，将学习到的GPR函数增广至运动学模型构成灰箱模型，以逼近动力学模型的精度，从而提升模型预测控制（MPC）的横向跟踪性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 矿用ADT自动驾驶需要精确车辆模型用于MPC、基于模型的估计与仿真。动力学模型精度高但建模与参数辨识复杂，且环境变化时需重新标定；运动学模型虽被MPC广泛采用，但因忽略侧偏角导致预测精度下降。作者识别侧偏角为运动学模型精度损失的主因，试图在不引入复杂动力学建模的前提下缩小与动力学模型的差距。

Method: 以运动学模型为骨架（白箱先验），使用高斯过程回归从数据中学习侧偏角这一残差项，并将其增广入运动学模型形成灰箱模型；基于该灰箱模型构建灰箱MPC。在仿真环境中与两个白箱MPC进行对比验证。

Result: 灰箱模型能够预测侧偏角，进而预测车辆横向速度，改善了MPC的预测性能。仿真对比显示，最大横向跟踪误差从超过2 m降低至0.56 m。

Conclusion: 将GPR学习得到的侧偏角增广到运动学模型中，可在保持运动学模型简洁性的同时显著提升ADT的MPC跟踪精度，为矿用车辆自动驾驶提供了一种介于运动学与动力学模型之间的折中建模方案。但验证仅限仿真，缺乏实车与真实工况证据。

Abstract: The growing demand for automation in the mining industry, particularly for the autonomous operation of articulated dump trucks (ADTs), has drawn increased attention to accurate vehicle modeling. The importance of such models lies in their use in model predictive control (MPC), model-based estimation methods, and vehicle simulation. While dynamic modeling offers a viable solution for these purposes, it is associated with complex setup and parametrization and may require recalibration in changing operating environments. As a result, kinematic models have dominated ADT modeling, especially in MPCs, at the expense of reduced prediction accuracy. In this work, we propose an approach using Gaussian Process Regression (GPR) to learn the sideslip angle of the vehicle, which is identified as the primary contributor to the reduced accuracy of kinematic models. The learned GPR function is augmented into the kinematic model to form a gray-box model that aims to reduce the gap to dynamic models. We show that the gray-box model can predict the sideslip angle and, consequently, the vehicle's lateral velocity, thereby improving the MPC's prediction performance. The resulting gray-box MPC is compared against two white-box MPCs in a simulation environment. The results indicate an improvement in terms of maximum lateral tracking error from over 2 m to 0.56 m.

</details>


### [157] [RegenHarness: A Robot Agent Harness with Evidence-Gated Recursive Self-Improvement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27612)
*Kailin Wang,Haoxiang Jie,Yaoyuan Yan,Zhiyou Heng,Zhaosong Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 RegenHarness——面向具身机器人智能体的"证据门控"执行与递归自我改进（RSI）框架，将模型提案、控制器终止与经验证的任务完成严格区分，并在真实四足机器人上完成语音触发仓储任务全链路演示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程机器人执行中，模型给出的"提案"、控制器的"终止信号"与真正"经验证的任务完成"常被混为一谈，导致终点接近即被误判为完成、重复派发、无法追溯的状态更新等问题；且现有智能体框架缺乏对自我改进的可信约束机制。

Method: 1）双层执行架构：模型循环（上下文条件提案）+ 智能体循环（派发、观测、验证、提交、有界恢复）；2）四个角色隔离上下文（规划/监督/验证/恢复）；3）版本化记忆区分"观测事实"与"已接受的任务进度"，提交门（commit gate）绑定身份与版本；4）运行时机制：重复派发抑制、资源租约、恢复预算、显式后端契约、报告完成前复核原始用户目标；5）首个面向具身智能体的证据门控 RSI 协议：执行记录驱动上下文规则、任务模板、路由与恢复策略的候选变更，经固定回归校验与发布授权方可接受，版本化 rollout/rollback 保证可追溯，禁止在线权重更新、禁止削弱提交门。

Result: 真实四足机器人部署完成语音触发仓储导航、全景巡检、视觉分析、消息投递、返回与语音汇报，并串联音频、图像、轨迹与回执；另一独立回路实验表明任务完成依赖执行历史而非仅端点接近度。

Conclusion: 案例整体验证了在真实机器人任务中集成感知、物理执行、通信与历史依赖式完成判定的可行性；证据门控 RSI 可在不更新模型权重、不放宽提交门的前提下持续修订 harness 配置。

Abstract: Long-horizon robot execution requires a clear distinction between a model's proposal, a controller's termination, and verified task completion. We present RegenHarness, an evidence-gated robot-agent harness connecting task planning to heterogeneous robot skills. Its execution architecture couples a model loop for context-conditioned proposals with an agent loop for dispatch, observation, verification, commitment, and bounded recovery. Four role-isolated contexts separate planning, supervision, verification, and recovery inputs. Versioned memory distinguishes observed facts from accepted task progress, while an identity- and version-bound commit gate controls updates to trusted task state. The runtime combines duplicate-dispatch control, resource leases, and recovery budgets under explicit backend contracts, and checks the original user goal before reporting completion. To our knowledge, we are the first to introduce an evidence-gated recursive self-improvement (RSI) protocol for embodied robotic agents. Across missions, execution records motivate candidate changes to context rules, task templates, routing, and recovery policies; fixed regression checks and release authorization govern their acceptance; versioned rollout and rollback preserve configuration traceability. This RSI protocol revises the harness configuration without online model-weight updates or permission to weaken the commit gate. A real quadruped deployment documents voice-triggered warehouse navigation, panoramic inspection, visual analysis, message delivery, return, and spoken reporting through linked audio, images, trajectories, and receipts. A separate circuit demonstrates why completion depends on execution history rather than endpoint proximity alone. Together, the cases demonstrate integrated perception, physical execution, communication, and history-dependent completion in real-world robot tasks.

</details>


### [158] [InternW0: A Foundational Physical World Model for Efficient Real-World Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27656)
*Jisong Cai,Yao Mu,Ganlin Yang,Zhe Cao,Zhangzheng Tu,Xing Gao,Kailin Li,Xinyu Zhan,Lixin Yang,Yangkun Zhu,Haoxiang Ma,Ming Zhou,Qiaojun Yu,Yufei Xue,Liqun He,Yifei Yao,Yifan Zhu,Long Ling,Bingqi Jiang,Haoyu Guo,Xueyue Zhu,Bowen Zhou,Bin Zhao,Tianfan Xue,Chunhua Shen,Weinan Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Shanghai AI Lab 提出 InternW0，首个 InternW 物理世界模型实例：以全模态接口、异步多频推理与局部物理建模为核心，通过非对称 video-action 架构（流匹配）联合学习未来视觉动态与连续机器人控制，用 layerwise K/V 复用与观测条件化上下文路由避免每步重生成未来，并经接触感知后训练引入力/触觉信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理智能不仅要求预测世界演化，还要求预测在世界持续变化时保持"可执行"。现有世界模型多为单一时间尺度、重生成式预测，在部分观测与外部影响下代价高、响应慢，且缺乏对异构本体与接触丰富操作（力/触觉）的支持。

Method: 非对称 video-action 架构 + flow matching：大容量 video expert 提供长时程预测上下文，轻量 action expert 在更快时间尺度运行；不逐步重生成未来，而是复用 layerwise K/V 并通过 observation-conditioned context routing 适配新观测。采用领域特定接口与 soft prompts 支持异构本体；contact-aware 后训练融合力与触觉信号。训练数据约 7,200 小时异构机器人与第一人称数据，含 275 小时真实实验室自采数据 EgoLab。

Result: 在仿真基准与真实科学任务上评估，包括 15 阶段金属—有机框架（MOF）合成流程，以及 5 阶段接触/力感知灵巧操作（通用定量移液），验证了异步、科学原生物理世界模型的可扩展性。

Conclusion: InternW0 为可扩展、异步、面向科学场景的物理世界模型提供了首个实例，推动通用且高效的真实世界交互；后续 InternW 系列将沿此路线扩展。

Abstract: Physical intelligence requires more than predicting how the world may evolve: predictions must remain actionable as the world continues to change. We introduce InternW0, the first instantiation of the InternW physical world model series from Shanghai AI Laboratory, built around omnimodal interfaces, asynchronous multi-frequency processing, and local physical modeling under partial observations and external influences. InternW0 jointly learns future visual dynamics and continuous robot control through an asymmetric video--action architecture with flow matching. A high-capacity video expert provides longer-horizon predictive context, while a lightweight action expert operates at a faster timescale. Instead of regenerating the future for every action update, InternW0 reuses layerwise K/V and adapts it to newly observed states through observation-conditioned context routing. Domain-specific interfaces and soft prompts support heterogeneous embodiments, while contact-aware post-training incorporates force and tactile signals for contact-rich manipulation. We train InternW0 on approximately 7,200 hours of heterogeneous robot and egocentric data, including EgoLab, a 275-hour real-laboratory egocentric dataset. Evaluation spans simulation benchmarks and real-world scientific tasks, including a 15-stage metal--organic framework synthesis workflow and 5-stage contact- and force-aware dexterous manipulation for general-purpose quantitative pipetting. These results advance scalable, asynchronous, and science-native physical world models for universal and efficient real-world interactions.

</details>


### [159] [GLoTouch: Global-to-Local Haptic Perception Using a Parallel Gripper for Object Search, Recognition, and Grasping Without External Vision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27695)
*Zonglin Li,Wanruo Zhang,Yiming Wang,Kun Song,Xinyi Zhou,Daolin Ma*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GLoTouch：基于平行夹爪的“全局—局部”触觉感知与操作框架，全局阶段用长臂探针做容器级空间探索，局部阶段用双侧视触觉传感器做物体级几何匹配与抓取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 暗光/低光下外部相机失效，需仅靠触觉完成目标搜索、识别与抓取；该任务同时涉及容器尺度空间探索与物体尺度精细几何感知，对低自由度平行夹爪尤为困难，而现有工作缺乏将两者统一连接的整体框架。

Method: 两阶段框架：(1) 全局阶段，夹爪夹持被动式长臂探针，结合力测量与已知工具几何定位接触点，主动估计候选物体位置、粗略轮廓与高度；(2) 局部阶段，放下探针，用同一夹爪上的双侧视触觉传感器获取局部触觉观测，并与给定目标三维模型匹配，无需物体专属训练。

Result: 在仿真与真实机器人实验中对框架进行了评估；源代码将开源。

Conclusion: 提出统一连接容器级空间探索与物体级几何感知、抓取的全局—局部触觉操作框架，验证了仅凭触觉在暗光环境下进行目标搜索、识别与抓取的可行性。

Abstract: Perceiving objects in the environment is a fundamental capability of autonomous robots. In dark or low-light environments, external cameras often fail to reliably perceive object positions and geometry; when visual sensing is unavailable, completing target search, recognition, and grasping through touch alone becomes a key robot manipulation capability. This task must simultaneously address container-scale spatial exploration and object-scale fine-grained geometric perception, which is particularly challenging for low-degree-of-freedom parallel grippers. However, a unified framework remains lacking for connecting container-scale spatial exploration with object-scale fine-grained geometric perception and grasping. To address this challenge, we present \textbf{GLoTouch}, a global-to-local haptic perception and manipulation framework built on a parallel gripper. In the global stage, the gripper holds a passive long-reach probe, combining force measurements with known tool geometry to localize contacts and actively estimate candidate-object positions, coarse contours, and heights. In the local stage, the robot sets down the probe and uses the bilateral visuotactile sensors on the same gripper to directly acquire local haptic observations, which are matched against a given target 3-D model without object-specific training. We evaluate the framework in both simulation and real-robot experiments. Source code will be open-sourced.

</details>


### [160] [DAVIO: Dense Monocular-Inertial SLAM with Feed-Forward Initialization and Pose-Conditioned Mapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27702)
*Jaafar Mahmoud,Arthur Movsesyan,Mikhail Iumanov,Sergey Kolyubin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DAVIO 用单个多视图深度模型 Depth Anything 3 同时承担初始化与建图，实现无需视差等待、即时的稠密度量 VIO/SLAM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相机+IMU 是最小度量定位与稠密建图配置，但传统 VIO 滤波需等待视差才能初始化且只保留稀疏路标；前馈几何模型虽能由少量图像预测稠密结构，却缺乏度量尺度与重力方向。论文旨在结合两者优势，解决启动慢、地图稀疏、无尺度的问题。

Method: 基于多视图深度模型 Depth Anything 3：启动阶段用五图像窗口加预积分 IMU 构成无特征线性系统，经鲁棒性与条件数检验后求解，通过缓冲回放引导 VIO 滤波器；跟踪阶段用滤波器的度量位姿对深度模型进行条件化，仅沿视线方向校正残余尺度以保持度量相机基线，并配合重力保持子图图优化与漂移门控回访精化地图。

Result: 在 EuRoC 上，DAVIO 初始化显著更早、定位误差更低，且在相同位姿下建图精度优于 SOTA 前馈建图器；在建筑尺度 ORI 序列上，同一里程计下与 SOTA 相当或更优，用真实里程计替换 GT 位姿时性能退化明显更小。作者开源了该实时稠密度量 SLAM 系统代码。

Conclusion: 单一多视图深度模型可统一初始化与建图，实现免特征、免视差等待的实时稠密度量 SLAM，且对里程计噪声鲁棒，具备开源与实时性等实用价值。

Abstract: A camera and an IMU are the minimal sensor setup for metric localization and dense mapping, yet classical visual--inertial filters must wait for parallax before they start and then retain only sparse landmarks. Feed-forward geometry models, in contrast, predict dense structure from a few images but provide neither metric scale nor gravity. We present DAVIO, which uses a single multi-view depth model, Depth Anything~3, for both start-up and mapping. At start-up, a five-image window and preintegrated IMU measurements form a feature-free linear system. Its robust, conditioning-checked solution bootstraps a VIO filter through buffered replay. During tracking, the filter's metric poses condition the depth model. Residual scale is corrected only along viewing rays, which preserves the metric camera baselines, and a gravity-preserving submap graph with drift-gated revisits refines the map. On EuRoC, DAVIO starts markedly earlier, reduces the localization error, and maps more accurately than SOTA feed-forward mappers given identical poses. On building-scale ORI sequences, DAVIO is on bar or better than SOTA mappers on the same odometry, and degrades far less when GT poses are replaced by real odometry. We release the code of DAVIO, a real-time dense metric SLAM system, to the community.

</details>


### [161] [Wave-Robust Passive AUV Localization Using FP-MUSIC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27712)
*Usama Saqib,Ola Rønning,Andrzej Wąsowski*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种接收端被动的三维水声定位与建图系统：用单个带水听器阵列和IMU的浮标，通过FP-MUSIC迭代算法利用IMU测量对快拍协方差去畸变，补偿波浪引起的6自由度阵列扰动，并结合子空间投影宽带匹配滤波与功率不对称前后向判别，实现无预置海底应答器条件下的AUV三维跟踪与姿态估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在不预置海底应答器、也无法直接访问AUV机载传感器的条件下实现AUV定位是核心难题。单浮标方案中，水面波浪运动导致阵列在快拍之间发生6自由度旋转扰动，破坏常规子空间处理（如MUSIC）所依赖的阵列流形假设，导致方位估计严重退化。

Method: 1) 引入定点迭代MUSIC算法（FP-MUSIC），利用IMU测得的姿态信息在DOA估计前对快拍协方差进行"去畸变"（de-warp），使阵列在等效空间域中重新对齐；2) 采用子空间投影的宽带匹配滤波器估计信标距离（时延/距离分辨）；3) 利用功率不对称性进行独立的前后向（front-back）模糊判别；整体为接收端被动、单浮标的三维定位与空间建图框架。

Result: 在多种模拟海况下的评估表明：相对于未补偿方法，FP-MUSIC显著降低定位误差；在波浪引起的运动下仍能维持稳健的三维跟踪与载体姿态估计。中等海况下，2米信标间隔的定位正确率由约45%提升至约75%。

Conclusion: 该工作表明将IMU姿态测量嵌入子空间DOA估计的迭代去畸变流程，可有效抑制波浪运动对单浮标水听器阵列的影响，从而在无预置海底应答器的条件下实现稳健的AUV被动三维定位与建图；后续需在真实海试与更多海况下验证。

Abstract: Localizing an autonomous underwater vehicle without pre-deployed seabed transponders, or direct access to onboard vehicle sensors remains a core challenge. We present a receiver-passive 3-D localization and spatial mapping system utilizing a single floating surface buoy equipped with a hydrophone array and an inertial measurement unit (IMU). The central difficulty is that surface wave motion induces six-degree-of-freedom (6-DOF) perturbations that rotate the array between snapshots, degrading conventional subspace processing. We resolve this by introducing a fixed-point iterative MUltiple SIgnal Classification algorithm (FP-MUSIC) that uses IMU measurements to de-warp snapshot covariances prior to direction-of-arrival estimation. Furthermore, we employ a subspace-projected wideband matched filter to resolve beacon ranges and use power asymmetry for independent front-back identification. Evaluations across simulated sea states demonstrate that FP-MUSIC substantially reduces localization error relative to uncompensated methods and sustains robust 3-D tracking and vehicle orientation estimation under wave-induced motion. At moderate sea state, FP-MUSIC increases the 2-m beacon-separation accuracy from approximately 45% to 75%.

</details>


### [162] [CoRelNav: Collaborative Relational Navigation for Multi-Robot Spatially Constrained Semantic Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27720)
*Jinyu He,Zihao Mao,Haonan Jin,Mengyin Fu,Wenjie Song*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CoRelNav：将任务条件化的多机器人探索与候选驱动的协同验证相耦合，用于空间约束的语义关系导航，并在仿真与两台实体移动机器人上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 关系感知导航（需按语义类别+与周围物体的关系指定目标）在未知环境中既需要高效探索，又需要足够的目标与上下文证据来验证关系。现有工作将"关系验证"与"多机器人协作"割裂：关系导航基本是单智能体，多机器人系统很少为实例级关系验证协同分布式观测，导致独立探索或单视角验证下关系假设仍存歧义。

Method: 核心是任务条件化多机器人探索与候选驱动协同验证的耦合。构建空间-语义场，将任务约束、场景节点和物体特征转换为探索效用；随候选信息累积，在团队导航代价约束下将机器人重分配以采集互补证据；并通过拓扑节点聚合实例一致的观测，用分布式部分证据消解关系假设。

Result: 在照片级真实感仿真中相对代表性基线取得一致性提升，消融实验验证了探索与验证两个机制的有效性；并在两台实体移动机器人上完整部署，展示真实世界协同导航的可用性。

Conclusion: 通过探索-验证耦合，减少冗余搜索，使分布式部分证据足以解除单智能体或孤立视角验证下难以判定的关系歧义，且方法可迁移至真实多机器人平台。

Abstract: Spatially constrained semantic navigation requires robots to identify targets specified not only by semantic categories but also by relations to surrounding objects. In unknown environments, resolving such goals requires efficient exploration together with sufficient target and contextual evidence for reliable relation verification. Existing methods leave relation-aware verification and multi-robot collaboration largely disconnected: relational navigation is predominantly single-agent, while multi-robot systems seldom coordinate distributed observations for instance-specific relation verification. We propose CoRelNav, whose core is coupling task-conditioned multi-robot exploration with candidate-driven collaborative verification. A spatial-semantic field converts task constraints, scene nodes, and object features into exploration utility; as candidate information accumulates, robots are reallocated toward complementary evidence under team navigation costs, while instance-consistent observations are aggregated across topology nodes. This coupling reduces redundant search and enables relation hypotheses to be resolved from distributed partial evidence that independent exploration or isolated-view verification can leave ambiguous. Experiments in photorealistic simulation demonstrate consistent improvements over representative baselines, with ablations validating the proposed exploration and verification mechanisms. We further deploy the complete system on two physical mobile robots, demonstrating its applicability to real-world collaborative navigation.

</details>


### [163] [InfiNoVA: Infinite Novel View Augmentation for Viewpoint Invariant Robot Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27734)
*Sai Puneeth Reddy Gottam,Elmar Rueckert,Vedant Dave*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: InfiNoVA将多相机示教重建为时变3D高斯表示，渲染稠密新视角以增强VLA训练，无需改策略结构即可显著提升未见视角鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA策略对训练相机视角依赖强，视角变化导致性能骤降；采集多视角物理示教成本高、覆盖稀疏。作者希望以数据增强方式获得稠密且几何一致的训练视角。

Method: 将每条操作轨迹重建为随时间变化的3D高斯表示（time-varying 3D Gaussian），从采样相机位姿渲染新观测，同时保持原始状态-动作对应关系；相比生成式新视角合成（如VISTA），显式场景表示提升帧级保真度与时间一致性，减少任务关键幻觉。

Result: 在4个真实操作任务上，未见随机视角下平均成功率比VISTA增强与未增强策略高5.4倍；比直接在5个物理相机视角上训练高1.7倍。

Conclusion: 稠密、几何落地的视角增强是提升机器人策略相机鲁棒性的可行路径，且无需修改底层策略架构。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) policies often rely strongly on the camera viewpoints seen during training, causing substantial performance degradation when deployed from unseen perspectives. Collecting demonstrations from sufficiently diverse physical viewpoints is expensive and still provides only sparse coverage of the viewpoint space. We introduce InfiNoVA, a data-augmentation framework that converts synchronized multi-camera demonstrations into a dense distribution of geometrically consistent training views. InfiNoVA reconstructs each manipulation trajectory as a time-varying 3D Gaussian representation and renders novel observations from sampled camera poses while preserving the original state-action correspondence. This explicit scene representation improves frame-level fidelity and temporal consistency while reducing task-critical hallucinations observed in generative novel-view synthesis. Across four real-world manipulation tasks, policies trained with InfiNoVA achieve 5.4x higher average success under unseen randomized viewpoints than both VISTA-based augmentation and the unaugmented policy. InfiNoVA further achieves 1.7x higher success than training directly on all five physical camera views. These results show that dense, geometrically grounded viewpoint augmentation provides a practical route toward camera-robust robot policies without modifying the underlying policy architecture.

</details>


### [164] [Less Language, More Latents: Annotation-Efficient VLAs for Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27747)
*Alexey Zakharov,Kemal Oksuz,Puneet K. Dokania*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: LADA提出三阶段流水线，用少于5%的语言标注，将无标注观测-轨迹对转化为语言条件控制的数据底座，训练驾驶VLA在Bench2Drive上达到DS 87.98、SR 70.46%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA用于可操控自动驾驶时受限于"帧-自然语言指令"配对数据的稀缺：相机流与专家轨迹可大规模采集，但语言标注（如"路口左转"）昂贵且稀少，成为训练瓶颈。

Method: 三阶段：1) 训练带向量量化瓶颈的潜在动作模型，得到高层驾驶意图的紧凑码本；2) 用少量语言标注子集训练视觉-语言翻译器，将观测与语言指令映射到该码本；3) 在全量无标注语料上以观测-潜在动作对训练驾驶VLA。不使用辅助链式推理或VQA数据流。

Result: 在闭环Bench2Drive上，仅用不到5%语言标注，取得Driving Score 87.98、Success Rate 70.46%，匹配或超过全监督基线。

Conclusion: 通过潜在动作码本作为语言与轨迹之间的中介，可将大量无标注驾驶数据转化为可语言条件的控制监督，显著降低语言标注依赖，为VLA数据瓶颈提供可扩展方案。

Abstract: Vision-language-action models (VLA) promise human-steerable autonomous driving, but their training is bottlenecked by the scarcity of frames paired with natural-language instructions: while camera streams and expert trajectories are logged at scale, language annotations (e.g., turn left at the intersection) remain scarce and expensive to acquire. To address this challenge, we introduce Latent Action Driving Annotations (LADA), a three-stage pipeline that transforms abundant unlabelled observation-trajectory pairs into a substrate for language-conditioned control. First, we train a latent action model with a vector-quantised bottleneck, producing a compact codebook of high-level vehicle intents. Second, a small language-annotated subset is used to train a vision-language translator to map observations and language instructions into this codebook. Third, we train a driving VLA on observation-latent-action pairs over the full unlabelled corpus. Using fewer than 5% of language annotations and without leveraging any auxiliary chain-of-thought reasoning or visual question answering streams, LADA achieves a Driving Score of 87.98 and a Success Rate of 70.46% on the closed-loop Bench2Drive benchmark, matching or surpassing fully supervised baselines.

</details>


### [165] [Task-Prototype Guided Flow Matching for Few-Shot Generalization in Vision-Language Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27780)
*Yizhao Wang,Guantao Zhang,Jingbo Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TP-Flow 将少量示教转化为结构化"任务原型"token，同时引导流匹配策略的初始先验与速度场，实现少样本视语言操作任务的快速适配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言指令存在欠指定性：接触时机、运动阶段、纠错行为与执行风格难以用语言表达，导致现有 VLA/操作策略难以仅凭少量示教迁移到新流程。

Method: 用对称交叉注意力与可学习 query 提取阶段级任务原型；参数化任务自适应初始分布作为流匹配先验；通过门控自适应归一化将原型注入速度场；采用 episodic support-query 训练目标与原型对比正则，抑制无关干扰信息。

Result: 在 LEROBOT-ARM-SO101 上 1/4/6-shot 成功率 66.8%/79.6%/82.1%，few-shot AUC 75.5%；1-shot 较 CFM、Pooled-Demo CFM、In-Context Flow 分别提升 29.8/14.5/9.9 个百分点；在novel-object transfer、goal recombination、长时程组合与接触纠错任务上泛化更好；6 个在线原型 token、54.3 ms 延迟、3.9 GB 峰值显存、10 Hz 控制；含噪示教下成功率下降仅 6.2%。

Conclusion: 原型引导的流匹配可在少样本下显著提升操作成功率与泛化，并保持实时性；理论诊断表明原型距离与动作分布距离对齐，自适应先验降低传输代价，门控调制将轨迹偏差控制在 ODE 界内。

Abstract: Vision-language robot manipulation policies can follow semantic instructions, but adapting them to a new procedure from only a few demonstrations remains difficult because language underspecifies contact timing, motion phases, corrective behavior, and execution style. This paper presents Task-Prototype Guided Flow Matching (TP-Flow), a few-shot manipulation framework that converts support demonstrations into structured task-prototype tokens and uses them to guide both the initial flow prior and the velocity field. TP-Flow employs symmetric cross-attention with learnable queries to extract phase-level prototypes, parameterizes a task-adaptive initial distribution, and injects prototype information through gated adaptive normalization. It is trained with an episodic support-query objective and prototype contrastive regularization, so few-shot adaptation is simulated during training while nuisance information is suppressed. On the LEROBOT-ARM-SO101 platform, TP-Flow achieves 66.8\%, 79.6\%, and 82.1\% success rates under 1-, 4-, and 6-shot settings, with a 75.5\% few-shot AUC. At 1-shot, it improves over CFM, Pooled-Demo CFM, and In-Context Flow by 29.8, 14.5, and 9.9 percentage points. It also improves held-out target-group generalization across novel-object transfer, goal recombination, long-horizon composition, and contact/correction tasks. TP-Flow maintains real-time execution with six online prototype tokens, 54.3 ms latency, 3.9 GB peak memory, and a 10 Hz control rate, while reducing the noisy-support success drop to 6.2\%. Theoretical diagnostics show that prototype distance aligns with action-distribution distance, the adaptive prior reduces transport cost, and gated modulation keeps measured trajectory deviations below the derived ODE bound. The code repository is omitted for anonymous review.

</details>


### [166] [Safe Multi-Robot Coordination via VLM-LLM Reasoning and Reachability Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27816)
*Mohamed Dwedar,Ahmad Hafez,Alexander Jesser,Amr Alanwar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出集中式安全感知异构M2M机器人协同导航框架：视觉四足机通过MQTT共享语义场景，VLM生成语义约束、LLM分配任务，机器人专属zonotope可达性门控验证命令后再执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异构M2M机器人团队中，各平台在感知能力、环境认知与运动执行角色上差异明显，无摄像头平台缺乏语义感知能力，且语义推理（VLM/LLM）输出不可靠，直接驱动物理执行存在安全风险，需要兼顾共享感知与可验证安全性。

Method: 视觉四足机器人将视觉流经VLM解析为语义信息，通过MQTT服务器集中共享给无摄像头车辆，并结合其里程计、IMU与状态反馈；语义数据映射为保守度量几何约束（膨胀障碍集、安全走廊、目标区域）；LLM提出高层任务分配，但物理命令权受机器人专属zonotope可达性门控约束，通过独立可达管传播验证避障、走廊容纳与机器人间分离谓词。

Result: 在清晰路径与动态障碍场景的在线实验中，管线能可靠批准安全运动，在约束违反时触发保守重规划或保持动作，并强制执行"建议性语义推理"与"形式化验证运动执行"之间的严格架构分离。

Conclusion: 共享感知+LLM任务分配+VLM语义约束+zonotope可达性验证的集中式框架，可在异构M2M团队中实现可验证的安全协同导航，验证层与语义层的架构分离是安全保障的关键。

Abstract: Safe coordination in heterogeneous machine-to-machine (M2M) robotic systems is challenging when robots differ in sensing capabilities, environmental awareness, and motion execution roles. This paper presents a centralized safety-aware M2M framework for cooperative goal-directed navigation in a heterogeneous mobile robot team comprising a vision-capable quadruped and a camera-less robotic vehicle. The objective is to guide both platforms toward a goal region while avoiding static and dynamic obstacles and preventing unsafe inter-robot interactions. Under the principle of shared perception, the vision-capable robot provides semantic environmental awareness through a centralized server over an MQTT broker, enabling the camera-less platform to navigate using this shared scene representation alongside its own odometry, IMU, and state feedback. A vision-language model (VLM) interprets the visual stream, and the extracted semantic data is mapped into conservative metric geometric constraints, including inflated obstacle sets, safe corridors, and goal regions. A large language model (LLM) proposes high-level task allocations, while physical command authority is restricted to a robot-specific zonotope reachability gate. This verification engine propagates independent reachable tubes to evaluate obstacle avoidance, safe-corridor containment, and inter-robot separation predicates before approving commands. Online experiments across clear-path and dynamic-obstacle scenarios show that the pipeline reliably approves safe motion, triggers conservative replanning or holding maneuvers upon constraint violation, and enforces a strict architectural separation between advisory semantic reasoning and formally verified motor execution.

</details>


### [167] [Anchor-Free Hidden-Target Seeking via Certified Self-Calibration under Correlated Odometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27905)
*Yash Bagla*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 在无全局位姿、单中继、仅距离-方位观测的锚点无约束场景下研究隐蔽目标搜索；证明绝对定位在SE(2)规范下不可解，但控制所需量可辨识，提出O(K)多视图自标定与一阶偏航不确定度证书，支撑混合搜索策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 隐蔽目标搜索通常依赖全局定位（车辆、中继、目标的已知位姿），但在GPS拒止、基础设施未标定、仅有机体里程计的场景下，全局位姿不可得。作者要回答一个根本问题：全局定位是否真的是可靠搜索的必要条件，以及在此退化条件下哪些量仍可辨识并足以驱动控制。

Method: 形式化单中继、仅距离-方位观测、中继位置与朝向未知、车辆运动仅由机体坐标系积分里程计给出的问题；证明联合构型存在精确的三维SE(2)规范自由度，绝对定位不可解；将标定约束到任务相关的商空间，得到中继-里程计偏航与目标位移，并给出两视图闭式辨识；提出O(K)多视图自标定估计器及一阶偏航不确定度证书，显式传播积分里程计引入的跨视图相关性；证书驱动混合策略（激励、拒绝未认证估计、持续重测几何、通过持续认证不一致检测中继坐标系变化）。

Result: 完整证书在标称95%置信下达到95.0%合并覆盖，忽略相关位姿独立性假设时仅82.1%；200次随机闭环试验中中位站点误差0.064 m，接近给定真实中继偏航的预言机0.065 m，尽管长时程中位航位推算漂移达27 m；90次基于物理的ROS 2/Gazebo试验在标称运行、通信退化、中继坐标系扰动下保持30/30成功率。

Conclusion: 在单中继隐蔽目标搜索模型下，全局定位并非可靠搜索的必要条件，即使感知基础设施与车辆自身参考系均未标定，任务相关量的可辨识性与带相关性的不确定度证书足以支撑稳定、可认证的搜索控制。

Abstract: We study hidden-target seeking in an anchor-free regime where neither the vehicle, the relay, nor the target has an accessible global pose. The vehicle never senses the target directly; it receives only range-bearing observations through a single relay of unknown position and orientation, with motion known only via integrated body-frame odometry. Absolute localization is fundamentally impossible: the joint configuration retains an exact three-dimensional SE(2) gauge no estimator can resolve. Yet the quantities needed for control remain fully recoverable: motion collapses calibration to a task-relevant quotient, the relay-to-odometry yaw and target displacement, identifiable in closed form from two distinct vehicle views. We introduce an O(K) multi-view self-calibration estimator with an exact first-order yaw-uncertainty certificate that propagates the cross-view correlations integrated odometry induces: the full certificate attains 95.0% pooled coverage at the nominal 95% level, versus 82.1% when correlated poses are treated as independent. The certificate drives a hybrid policy that excites until calibration is trustworthy, refuses uncertified estimates, seeks using continuously re-measured geometry, and detects relay-frame changes via persistent certified inconsistency, avoiding unbounded dead-reckoning drift. Across 200 randomized closed-loop trials, the method attains 0.064 m median station error versus 0.065 m for an oracle given the true relay yaw, despite 27 m median dead-reckoning drift over long horizons; 90 physics-based ROS 2/Gazebo trials retain 30/30 success under nominal operation, communication degradation, and relay-frame disturbances. These results show global localization is unnecessary for reliable hidden-target seeking under this single-relay model, even when both the sensing infrastructure and the vehicle's own reference frame are uncalibrated.

</details>


### [168] [Remote Surfaces at Your Fingertips: Electrovibration-Based Tactile Feedback for Robot Teleoperation via Touchscreen Interfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27938)
*Alperen Kenan,Juan José García Cárdenas,Adriana Tapus,Paul Bremner,Manuel Giuliani*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于电振动（electrovibration）触觉反馈的遥操作接口，用指尖触觉再现远端表面接触力，规避力觉反馈的稳定性与延迟问题；N=21用户实验显示相比视觉反馈响应时间缩短15.35%、临场感提升31%，工作负荷与可用性相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 接触密集型遥操作中，操作者需要实时触觉感知以提升安全性与作业效率。现有力觉（kinesthetic）反馈在刚性表面接触时易失稳且对通信延迟敏感；基于视觉提示的力反馈则增加认知负荷、削弱持续情境感知。因此需要一种既能自然映射力反射、又不受稳定性与延迟约束的反馈模态。

Method: 构建以电振动触觉反馈传递远端表面交互的遥操作界面，实现"自然映射"的力反射；开展两项实验：（1）被试内用户研究（N=21），在视觉反馈与电振动触觉反馈两种条件下测量可用性、临场感与操作负荷；（2）特性表征实验，测量路径跟随精度与响应时间。

Result: 相比视觉反馈，触觉反馈使响应时间显著降低15.35%（p=0.002, d=0.96），临场感提升31%（p<0.001, d=0.90）；两种条件下的工作负荷与可用性无显著差异。效应量均接近大效应区间。

Conclusion: 电振动触觉反馈是机器人遥操作中可行且有效的力反射模态，能提升接触丰富操作任务中的操作者响应性与临场感，适用于核维护等安全关键场景；但证据限于单侧触觉接口与统计性用户指标，缺乏真实机器人任务完成度与长期部署验证。

Abstract: Enabling operators to perceive and interact with remote environments naturally is a fundamental challenge in robotic teleoperation. This is especially critical in tasks involving physical interaction, where real-time haptic awareness improves operational safety and effectiveness. Existing kinesthetic haptic feedback methods suffer from instability during rigid surface contacts and remain sensitive to communication delays, while visual cue-based force feedback imposes additional cognitive load and limits sustained situational awareness. This work presents a teleoperation interface that conveys remote surface interactions to the operator through electrovibration-based tactile feedback, enabling naturally mapped force reflection while avoiding the stability issues associated with kinesthetic feedback and the latency limitations of mechanical actuators. A user study (N=21) evaluated interface usability, sense of presence, and operator workload under two force reflection conditions: visual feedback and electrovibration-based tactile feedback. Characterisation experiments further assessed path-following accuracy and response time across both conditions. Results show that tactile feedback significantly reduced response time by 15.35% (p=0.002, d=0.96) and increased the sense of presence by 31% (p<0.001, d=0.90) compared to visual feedback, while imposing comparable workload and usability across both conditions. These findings demonstrate that electrovibration-based tactile feedback is a viable and effective modality for robot teleoperation, improving operator responsiveness and sense of presence in contact-rich manipulation tasks, with direct applicability to safety-critical domains such as nuclear maintenance.

</details>


### [169] [Learning a Speed-adaptive Hip Exoskeleton Control Policy Via Sim-to-real Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28027)
*Bin Li,Zhimin Hou,Jiacheng Hou,Zenian Liang,Tong Wu,Teng Ma,Chenglong Fu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该工作将仿真到现实的强化学习与在线偏好学习结合，实现不同步行速度下的个性化髋关节外骨骼助力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有在线优化方法样本效率低，需要大量人在回路评估来优化整个助力扭矩曲线；而仿真到现实RL虽高效却无法直接捕捉个体用户偏好。

Method: 在仿真中用人体肌骨模型、跨多种步行速度训练RL策略学习助力时序；将策略蒸馏并基于机载传感观测部署到实体髋外骨骼；再用高斯过程偏好学习，通过用户两两比较个性化助力幅值。通过解耦仿真中的时序学习与真实实验中的幅值优化，大幅缩小在线优化空间。

Result: 人体被试实验表明，在更少的真实世界评估次数下即可高效识别跨不同步行速度的个性化助力扭矩曲线。

Conclusion: 仿真到现实RL与在线偏好学习的解耦框架能显著降低外骨骼个性化的在线优化负担，实现高效、跨速度的个性化助力。

Abstract: Providing personalized exoskeleton assistance across varying walking speeds remains challenging. Existing online optimization methods are sample-inefficient, requiring extensive human-in-the-loop (HIL) evaluations to optimize the entire assistive torque profile. Sim-to-real reinforcement learning (RL) offers a promising alternative but cannot directly account for individual user preferences. We propose a framework integrating sim-to-real RL with online preference learning for personalized exoskeleton assistance. Specifically, assistance timing is learned in simulation by training RL policies with human musculoskeletal models across varying walking speeds. The learned policies are then distilled and deployed on a physical hip exoskeleton using onboard sensory observations. Gaussian-process-based preference learning further personalizes the assistance magnitude through pairwise user comparisons. By decoupling assistance timing learning in simulation from magnitude optimization in real-world experiments, our framework substantially reduces the online optimization space. Human-subject experiments demonstrate efficient identification of personalized assistive torque profiles across varying walking speeds with fewer real-world evaluations.

</details>


### [170] [AeRSoM: An Aerial Rigid-Soft Integrated Manipulator for Contact-Rich Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28044)
*Jiacheng Liang,Hang Zhong,Yaonan Wang,Ge Chen,Zhixing Zhang,Bocheng Tian,Hui Zhang,Li Wen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出刚柔集成空中机械臂机器人AeRSoM，通过全驱动飞行平台+刚柔机械臂+变刚度调节实现"具身柔顺性"，并设计复合控制框架实现末端精确轨迹跟踪，在接触密集的空中作业任务中验证了鲁棒性与精度兼顾的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 接触密集的空中作业中，交互力会直接传递到飞行平台，导致不稳定与任务性能退化；柔性机械臂虽能缓解冲击，但现有系统难以同时兼顾交互柔顺性与操作精度。因此需要一种能在全系统层面协调柔顺与刚度的设计方案。

Method: 提出AeRSoM刚柔集成空中机械臂：集成全驱动空中平台、刚柔耦合机械臂与变刚度调节机构，将柔顺性分布到整个操作系统中以被动吸收接触扰动，同时保持任务执行所需的刚度；并开发复合控制框架，在存在不确定性与外部扰动时实现末端执行器的精确轨迹跟踪。

Result: 在多种真实接触密集空中作业任务中验证，包括动态输电线抓取、风机叶片物理交互、轴孔装配与拧螺丝操作；结果表明刚柔集成显著提升了交互鲁棒性与任务适应性，同时保持操作精度。

Conclusion: 具身柔顺性（embodied compliance）是提升空中作业安全性、鲁棒性与通用性的有前景的设计范式；刚柔集成可在不牺牲精度的前提下改善接触交互表现。

Abstract: Contact-rich aerial manipulation remains fundamentally challenging because interaction forces are directly transmitted to the aerial platform, often leading to instability and degraded task performance. While compliant manipulators can mitigate these effects, existing aerial manipulation systems typically struggle to reconcile interaction compliance with manipulation precision. To this end, this article presents an aerial rigid-soft integrated manipulator (AeRSoM) robot that realizes embodied compliance for aerial manipulation. The proposed system integrates a fully actuated aerial platform, a rigid-soft manipulator, and variable-stiffness regulation to simultaneously achieve stable flight, compliant interaction, and precise manipulation. By distributing compliance throughout the manipulation system, the proposed design leverages distributed embodied compliance to passively absorb contact disturbances while preserving sufficient stiffness for task execution. To fully exploit the mechanical design, a composite control framework is developed for precise end-effector trajectory tracking in the presence of uncertainties and external disturbances. Extensive real-world experiments are conducted in representative contact-rich aerial manipulation tasks, including dynamic transmission-line grasping, physical interaction with a wind turbine blade, peg-in-hole, and screwing operations. The results demonstrate that the proposed rigid-soft integration significantly improves interaction robustness and task adaptability while maintaining manipulation accuracy, highlighting that embodied compliance provides a promising design paradigm for enhancing the safety, robustness, and versatility of aerial manipulation.

</details>


### [171] [Distillation for Efficient Multitask Manipulation Policies via Conditional Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28107)
*Shreya Deshmukh,Imen Mahdi,Nick Heppert,Abhinav Valada*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 将多个单任务条件流匹配（CFM）专家的速度场蒸馏进共享多任务策略，并与原始CFM目标联合训练，在RLBench上以固定模型规模提升多任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单任务训练策略计算代价高，直接拼接多任务数据训练需增大模型容量或导致性能下降，故需在固定容量下实现多任务生成式策略学习。

Method: 以单任务CFM专家为教师，将学习到的速度场（velocity field）蒸馏至共享多任务学生策略，蒸馏损失与原始CFM回归目标加权结合以保持对示范数据的保真度。

Result: 在RLBench上，所提方法优于朴素多任务训练，且在模型规模不变的前提下提升多任务策略表现。

Conclusion: 速度场蒸馏可有效缓解多任务CFM策略的容量-性能权衡，为多任务生成式模仿学习提供轻量方案。

Abstract: Advances in generative modeling have recently been extensively employed in robotics for policy learning. In particular, Conditional Flow Matching (CFM) trained with expert demonstrations has been shown to outperform existing methods on robot manipulation benchmarks. While prior work has mainly focused on single-task settings, we study the problem from a multi-task perspective, as training independent models for each task is computationally expensive.
