Multi-Task policy learning comes with its own set of challenges, as naively training on a concatenated dataset of demonstrations would either require increased model capacity to accommodate the added complexity or result in drops in performance. We propose to distill knowledge from single-task CFM experts into a shared multi-task policy by transferring their learned velocity fields. We combine this distillation signal with the original CFM objective to retain fidelity to the demonstrations. Experiments on RLBench show that our approach improves multi-task policy performance over naive training while maintaining a fixed model size.

</details>


### [172] [DEAL-Grasp: Decoupled Alignment Representation for Geometry-Aware Dexterous Grasp Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28131)
*Fuqiang Zhao,Qian Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DEAL-Grasp 提出将灵巧抓取合成从耦合关节空间重构为"对齐空间"生成问题：以任务空间几何锚点+关节参数为混合状态，用异构状态流匹配（分量独立向量场）生成，推理时仅积分向量场即可，无需测试时优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有灵巧抓取合成方法在耦合了全局刚体运动与局部关节的联合空间中做回归或去噪，导致样本不稳定、接触物理不可行。作者希望解耦刚体变换与局部关节，使生成过程更稳定、接触更合理。

Method: 基于 Decoupled Alignment (DEAL) 表示：交互状态由任务空间几何锚点与关节参数组成，刚体变换通过对齐（Procrustes 闭式解）恢复，同时保留局部关节。在该混合状态上用异构状态流匹配训练，各分量使用各自的向量场；训练时引入时间自适应物理正则化。推理阶段仅积分学习到的向量场，不做测试时优化或额外物理引导。

Result: 在 MultiDex 与零样本 RealDex 基准上，DEAL-Grasp 取得高力扰动成功率、极小的穿透量与高生成多样性，同时相比优化密集型基线显著降低原生推理延迟。

Conclusion: 将抓取合成重构为对齐空间生成、并配合流匹配与训练期物理正则，能在无需推理期优化的情况下兼顾物理可行性与效率，说明解耦表示是灵巧抓取生成的有效归纳偏置。

Abstract: Synthesizing realistic articulated hand-object interactions is a fundamental problem in virtual reality, embodied intelligence, and digital human applications. Existing methods for dexterous grasp synthesis typically regress or denoise poses in a joint space that couples global rigid motion with local articulation, which often yields unstable samples and physically implausible contacts. We introduce DEAL-Grasp, built upon the Decoupled Alignment (DEAL) representation, which reformulates grasp synthesis as alignment-space generation: the interaction state comprises task-space geometric anchors and articulation parameters, from which the rigid transform is recovered via closed-form Procrustes alignment while preserving local articulation. On this mixed state, we model grasp generation using heterogeneous-state flow matching with component-wise vector fields, incorporating time-adaptive physical regularization during training. At inference, grasps are synthesized solely by integrating the learned vector field, without test-time optimization or auxiliary physical guidance. Across MultiDex and zero-shot RealDex benchmarks, DEAL-Grasp attains high force-perturbation success rates alongside minimal penetration and high diversity of generated grasps, while substantially reducing native inference latency compared to optimization-heavy baselines. The project page is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [173] [Dissecting Advantage-Guided Post-Training for Vision-Language-Action Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28161)
*Jiahang Cao,Hanye Zhao,Hang Lai,Shenyu Zhang,Xiaoshen Han,Xinghang Li,Futeng Liu,Wanli Peng,Heyun Wang,Yunhong Wang,Jason Li,Yong Yu,Weinan Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文通过受控实证研究解耦 advantage-guided VLA 后训练中的设计选择，提出分阶段离线评估方法以高效筛选方案，得出"时序差分优势构造 + 组内校准 + 连续优势加权"的模块化配方，在四个真实双臂任务上显著超越 SFT 初始化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: advantage-guided RL 是使用有限机器人数据后训练 VLA 策略的实用方法，但其性能取决于多个耦合选择（优势构造、校准、策略训练中的使用方式）。现有方法将这些选择捆绑为端到端流程，难以孤立识别各选项的影响，且为每种组合做真实机器人评估成本极高。

Method: 开展受控实证研究，将 advantage-guided VLA 后训练的设计选择分离，并考虑其不同的估计目标（estimand）；提出分阶段离线评估方法（stage-specific offline evaluation），在无需大量真机策略评估的前提下高效筛选替代设计；据此组合出模块化配方：时序差分优势构造、组内（group-wise）校准、连续优势加权。

Result: 在四个真实双臂（bimanual）任务上，该配方相较 SFT 初始化平均任务进度提升 0.42、成功率提升 0.63；所提出的评估诊断指标与下游真实世界性能整体一致。

Conclusion: advantage-guided VLA 后训练的设计选择可被解耦并独立评估；分阶段离线诊断可作为实际中解释实验结果、筛选后训练设计的有效工具。

Abstract: Advantage-guided reinforcement learning provides a practical way to post-train vision-language-action (VLA) policies using limited robot data. However, its performance depends on several coupled choices, including how critic-derived advantages are constructed, calibrated, and used for policy training. Existing recipes often combine these choices into a single end-to-end procedure, making their individual effects difficult to identify. In this work, we dissect advantage-guided VLA post-training through a controlled empirical study that separates these design choices while accounting for their distinct estimands. We develop stage-specific offline evaluation methods to screen alternative choices efficiently, without requiring extensive real-robot policy evaluations for every possible combination. The staged evaluation identifies a modular recipe that combines temporal-difference advantage construction, group-wise calibration, and continuous advantage weighting. Across four real-world bimanual tasks, the resulting recipe improves mean task progress and success over the SFT initialization by 0.42 and 0.63, respectively. Moreover, the proposed evaluation diagnostics show an overall alignment with downstream real-world performance, supporting their use for interpreting empirical outcomes and selecting advantage-guided post-training designs in practice.

</details>


### [174] [Dynamic, Decentralized Spatial Code Reuse for OCDMA LiDAR in Robot Swarms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28172)
*Mohammad Hani Alomari*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出去中心化协议，基于实时信标维护的干扰邻域图动态重分配空间复用码，证明常密度下所需码数为O(log N/log log N)，仿真验证复用效率与碰撞风险优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LiDAR群体中光测距码碰撞导致相互干扰；现有方案仅静态分配（需L=N个码）或对已检测干扰被动反应，缺乏可扩展的协调分配规则，码分配的可扩展性问题未解。

Method: 去中心化协议：机器人依据信标维护的实时干扰邻域图动态重分配空间复用码；在常密度假设下证明码数上界为O(log N/log log N)；用30个随机种子的蒙特卡洛仿真（95%置信区间）在机器人移动、不完美信标检测、反应式重分配等条件外验证；并与结构忠实、公平构建的现有无协调方法模型对比。

Result: 码数增长O(log N/log log N)相对静态分配的Θ(N)为无界改进；相对静态分配优势从15机器人约2×扩大到120机器人12×；相同受限码预算下，相对现有无协调方法实现更高码复用效率与30–40%更低碰撞风险。

Conclusion: 协调（而非单纯反应式）才是缩小码分配扩展性差距的关键；所提去中心化协议在非理想条件下仍保持理论增益。

Abstract: Robots in a LiDAR-equipped swarm mutually interfere when their optical ranging codes collide. Existing mitigations either assign codes statically -- requiring $L=N$ distinguishable codes for $N$ robots -- or react to detected interference without a scalable, coordinated assignment rule beneath them; prior work explicitly identifies the code-assignment scaling problem as unsolved. We propose a decentralized protocol in which robots dynamically reassign spatial reuse codes based on a live, beacon-maintained interference-neighborhood graph, and prove that the number of codes required grows as $O(\log N/\log\log N)$ under constant robot density -- an unbounded improvement over the $\Theta(N)$ growth of static assignment. We validate this result under conditions substantially beyond the idealized proof -- robot mobility, imperfect beacon-based detection, and reactive reassignment -- via Monte Carlo simulation (30 seeds per condition, 95% confidence intervals): the advantage over static assignment widens from roughly $2\times$ at 15 robots to $12\times$ at 120. Against a structurally faithful, fairly constructed model of an existing coordination-free approach, our protocol achieves both substantially greater code-reuse efficiency and 30--40% lower collision risk under an identical, constrained code budget, demonstrating that coordination -- not merely reactivity -- is what closes the scaling gap.

</details>


### [175] [DAVIS: A Depth-Only End-to-End Active-Vision Framework for Humanoid Soccer Skills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28175)
*Jiakang Jin,Yixiao Huo,Pengyuan Wang,Yinan Han,Tingxuan Zhang,Zhuobing Zhao,Xuanxin Zhou,Zhangchen Ye,Enxuan Ruan,Yifei Bao,Jiankun Yang,Chenghao Sun,Wenhao Cui,Xiaoyu Tian,Yiming Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出DAVIS：仅用头部深度图+本体感知历史的端到端人形足球接触技能框架，训练期用可见性感知几何辅助任务、GT-to-prediction退火、任务课程与AMP运动先验桥接特权监督与实机部署，直接输出25自由度关节PD目标，在Noetix E1真机上验证射门与带球。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形足球接触技能（射门、带球）要求同时闭环感知、接近、对准、撞击与恢复；机器人自身运动造成视角剧变、目标频繁出视野、接触结果不确定。现有方法多依赖额外运行时感知/规划模块或高冲击力开环控制，问题在于：能否仅凭头部深度图像、本体感知历史与可选低维任务指令，端到端输出关节PD目标完成接触技能。

Method: DAVIS为纯深度端到端框架：训练时学习可见性感知的辅助几何表征（visibility-aware auxiliary geometry），叠加GT-to-prediction退火（教师特权信息逐步替换为策略自身预测）、任务课程（task curricula）与AMP风格运动先验以平滑过渡特权监督到真机部署；以任务特定的物体、命令、奖励与课程定义实例化射门与定向带球两个代表性技能，输出25-DoF关节PD目标，无额外运行时感知或规划模块。

Result: 在仿真、Noetix E1真机实验及消融中验证：完成目标导向射门与方向性带球技能，并考察辅助几何、退火、课程与运动先验各组件的贡献（摘要未给出定量指标）。

Conclusion: 深度仅输入的端到端学习可在人形上实现接触类足球技能，并借助特权监督退火与运动先验实现sim-to-real迁移；为无需运行时规划模块的人形接触技能提供了可行范式。

Abstract: Humanoid soccer contact skills require more than producing high-impact foot-ball contacts: the robot must close the loop over perception, approach, alignment, impact, and recovery while its own motion induces substantial viewpoint changes, frequent loss of the ball from view, and uncertain contact outcomes. In this work, we ask a compact yet stricter question: can a humanoid learn soccer contact skills using only a head-mounted depth image, proprioceptive history, and an optional low-dimensional task command, and directly output 25-DoF joint PD targets without extra runtime perception or planning modules? To this end, we propose DAVIS, a depth-only end-to-end framework for humanoid soccer skills that learns visibility-aware auxiliary geometry during training, and combines GT-to-prediction annealing, task curricula, and AMP-style motion priors to smoothly bridge privileged supervision and real deployment. Built on this framework, we instantiate representative soccer contact skills, including goal-directed shooting and directional dribbling, through task-specific definitions of objects, commands, rewards, and curricula, and validate them through simulation, Noetix E1 real-robot experiments, and ablations.

</details>


### [176] [GLASS: Architecture-Tuned, Composable, Device-Side Linear Algebra for Edge Robotics and Beyond](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28179)
*Brian Plancher*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GLASS: a header-only CUDA C++ library unifying thread/warp/block/NVIDIA-backed implementations of robotics linear algebra and geometry under one composable device API, with compile-time static resolution of implementation choice, execution scope, and launch packing. Placement choice matters (median 4.9x, max 81x spread), and optimal choices differ across GPUs (145/396 change Orin vs RTX 5090). Up to 73x over best-of-PyTorch/JAX on Jetson AGX Orin; integration improved embedded robotics runtimes up to 1.5x and uncovered a numerical bug.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GPU robotics lacks reusable numerical infrastructure: it relies on compiler frameworks with overhead, or reimplements numerical libraries. Optimal placement of computation (thread/warp/block/device scope) is architecture-specific and highly consequential, yet not systematically exploited.

Method: Header-only CUDA C++ library (GLASS) providing composable device API covering thread-, warp-, block-, and NVIDIA-library-backed linear algebra and geometric primitives. Implementation choice, execution scope, and launch packing are treated as placement decisions determined by offline measurement and resolved statically at compile time. Released open source with independent numerical oracles and source-bound local-GPU test attestation.

Result: Best vs worst placement differ by median 4.9x (max 81x); 145/396 recommended placements shift between Jetson AGX Orin and RTX 5090, 162/396 vs AGX Xavier. Advantage over best of PyTorch/JAX up to 73x on Orin vs 12x on RTX 5090 (edge advantage largest). Integration with published robotics systems exposed a pre-existing numerical bug and improved embedded runtimes up to 1.5x.

Conclusion: Architecture-specific, compile-time-resolved placement is a first-order design axis for GPU robotics numerics; GLASS provides reusable, attested infrastructure with large edge-device wins for embedded robotics.

Abstract: GPU robotics lacks the reusable numerical infrastructure of mature CPU stacks, instead relying on compiler frameworks that introduce overhead or repeatedly reimplementing numerical libraries. To address this, we introduce GLASS (GPU Linear Algebra Simple Subroutines), a header-only CUDA C++ library that provides thread-, warp-, block-, and NVIDIA-backed implementations of robotics-scale linear algebra and geometric computations under one composable device API. GLASS treats implementation choice, execution scope, and launch packing as architecture-specific placement decisions determined by offline measurement and resolved statically at compile time. This is critical as the best and worst placements differ by a median of 4.9x (max 81x), with 145 of 396 recommended placements changing between a Jetson AGX Orin and an RTX 5090, and 162 of 396 versus an AGX Xavier. These stakes are highest at the edge as GLASS's advantage over the best of PyTorch and JAX is as much as 73x on the Orin versus 12x on the RTX 5090. GLASS is released open source with independent numerical oracles and source-bound local-GPU test attestation. Finally, integrating GLASS with published robotics systems both exposed a pre-existing numerical bug and improved embedded runtimes by up to 1.5x.

</details>


### [177] [VLMs Can Describe, But Not Measure: Object-Centric Scene Understanding for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28184)
*Enrico Saccon,Tommaso Faraci,Iñigo De La Ossa Zarzuelo,Luigi Palopoli,Marco Roveri,Matteo Saveriano*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出VLM驱动的模块化场景感知框架：单帧RGB-D经分割、VLM标注与深度接地，构建任务无关的物体中心表示，在151个桌面场景中语义性能保持且定位与深度估计显著优于直接VLM推理，并接入任务规划用于机器人执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM具备强语义能力但几何估计（定位、深度）不可靠，而机器人操作既需要语义理解又需要可靠的度量信息，因此需要一种能结合两者优势的模块化感知方案。

Method: 以单次RGB-D观测为输入，先用现成分割方法得到物体级区域，再由VLM对区域进行语义标注，并将标注与深度信息接地（grounding），从而构建任务无关的物体中心表示；整体为模块化、使用现成组件的VLM驱动框架，并可与任务规划框架集成。

Result: 在151个桌面场景上的实验表明，该分解方式在保持较强语义性能的同时，相较直接VLM推理显著提升了定位与深度估计精度；所得表示成功接入任务规划框架用于机器人执行。

Conclusion: 将语义与几何解耦的模块化设计可有效弥补VLM几何估计的不足，为机器人操作提供兼顾语义与度量的物体中心表示，并可直接服务于下游任务规划。

Abstract: Robotic operation in previously unseen environments requires both semantic understanding and reliable metric information. While vision--language models (VLMs) provide strong semantic capabilities, their geometric estimates remain less reliable. In this paper, we propose a VLM-driven, modular perception framework for scene understanding using off-the-shelf approaches. Starting from a single RGB-D observation, the scene is segmented into object-level regions, annotated by a VLM, and grounded with depth information to construct a task-independent object-centric representation. Experiments on 151 tabletop scenes show that the proposed decomposition preserves strong semantic performance while substantially improving localization and depth estimation over direct VLM inference. The resulting representation is also integrated with a task-planning framework for robotic execution.

</details>


### [178] [Large-Scale Geometric Map-Based Localization of UAVs in GNSS-Denied Urban Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28225)
*Garth Terlizzi,Kaveh Fathian*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于建筑轮廓几何关系的视觉定位方法：无人机下视图像检测建筑，跨帧聚合为局部地图，用增强三角结构与单体形状特征的几何描述子匹配参考轮廓库，实现GNSS拒止下大范围定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GNSS拒止的城市环境中无人机需要替代定位手段；现有卫星影像检索或学习描述子依赖视觉外观，对外观变化敏感，且随搜索区域增大性能迅速退化。

Method: 从下视航拍图像中检测建筑；将多帧观测累积为统一局部地图；设计几何驱动描述子，在局部三角结构基础上融合单体建筑形状特征，对邻近建筑的空间关系（而非视觉外观）进行编码，与参考建筑轮廓数据库匹配实现定位。

Result: 在大都市区四个市镇的七次飞行中，搜索区域约113 km²与254 km²均达100% Recall@1，扩展至约452 km²（含约27.7万栋建筑）时71.4% Recall@1；基线方法在254 km²和452 km²时Recall@1降至0%。

Conclusion: 以建筑空间几何关系替代视觉外观可显著提升大搜索区域下的定位鲁棒性与可扩展性，验证了轮廓级几何描述子在GNSS拒止城市无人机定位中的有效性。

Abstract: Unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) operating in GNSS-denied urban environments require alternative methods for position estimation. Existing approaches based on satellite image retrieval or learned descriptors are sensitive to appearance variation and degrade rapidly as the search area grows. We present a vision-based localization system that matches building patterns observed from a downward-facing UAV camera against a reference building footprint database. Our approach detects buildings in aerial imagery, accumulates observations across frames into a unified map, and matches local building arrangements against reference footprints using a novel geometry-driven descriptor that augments local triangle structure with per-building shape features. By encoding spatial relationships between nearby buildings rather than visual appearance, the system is robust to appearance variations and remains discriminative over large search areas. Evaluations on seven flights across four municipalities in a large metropolitan area demonstrate 100% Recall@1 at search areas of approximately 113 km$^2$ and 254 km$^2$, and 71.4% Recall@1 when expanded to approximately 452 km$^2$, encompassing up to 277,000 buildings. In contrast, baseline methods degrade rapidly and achieve 0% Recall@1 at 254 km$^2$ and 452 km$^2$.

</details>


### [179] [Controlling Collectives of AI Agents in Reasoning Space with Spatial Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28247)
*Frederic Vatnsdal,Roshan Gopal,Romina Garcia Camargo,Vijay Kumar,Alejandro Ribeiro*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 COMPASS：一种去中心化、可扩展的多机器人架构，用空间 Transformer 生成"推理反馈 token"实现反馈控制，可零样本泛化到 1024 台机器人的集体编队飞行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 为机器人规划与导航带来新范式，但在多机器人任务中随团队规模增长迅速失效，缺乏可扩展的分散式控制与有效的群体间通信/反馈机制。

Method: COMPASS：完全去中心化架构，每台机器人本地由空间 Transformer 聚合多跳（multi-hop）邻域消息，压缩为学习到的反馈 token 作为推理空间反馈信号；语言模型群体使用结构化多样化的指令输入以抵消偏差；对比集中式前沿 LLM 策略与纯语言通信消融，以及手工设计反馈（原始状态进入语言通道）的消融。

Result: 结构化多样性指令带来跨规模的性能增益；相比集中式前沿 LLM 策略和纯语言通信，COMPASS 的耦合设计显著产生内聚编队并准确按意图飞行；推理反馈与紧凑学习 token 组合效果最佳；手工反馈（原始状态入语言通道）破坏内聚性；零样本泛化到歧义指令，外推到 16 倍训练规模、最多 1024 台机器人。

Conclusion: 紧凑学习反馈 token + 局部多跳聚合是 LLM 驱动大规模机器人集群的关键设计；分散式架构比集中式 LLM 更可扩展且更能保持编队内聚。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) introduce an exciting new paradigm for planning and navigation in robotics, but fail on even simple multi-robot tasks as team sizes grow. We propose COMPASS, a scalable, decentralized multi-robot architecture for controlling large collectives of agentic robots with reasoning space feedback control. Feedback is generated locally on each robot by a spatial transformer which aggregates multi-hop messages across the fleet into a learned feedback token. Our experiments find that collectives of language models demonstrate performance gains from structured diversity of the input command, which can cancel biases; an advantage that is held across scale. Compared against a centralized frontier LLM policy and a language-only communication ablation, we find that the coupled design of COMPASS decisively produces cohesive flocking formations that accurately fly the commanded intent. We show that reasoning feedback works best when composed with a compact learned token. Our ablations show that hand engineered feedback with raw state appearing in the language channel obliterates cohesion. COMPASS generalizes zero-shot to unseen instructions of ambiguous meaning while commanding flocks up to 16 times its training scale, flying up to 1024 robots under natural language commands.

</details>


### [180] [MemBodied: Recurrent Associative Memory for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28256)
*Tej Deep Pala,Navonil Majumder,Bryce Goh,Raphael Yee,Jianfei Yang,Liming Chen,Soujanya Poria*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 MemBodied：固定尺寸情节记忆（关联状态 + 情节锚点），在每次策略调用时以当前观测与记忆为条件生成动作，替代扩张式上下文，在 RMBench 记忆任务上将成功率提升至无状态策略的 7.81 倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLA 策略多为无状态或仅依赖当前观测，无法利用仅在历史观测中出现的信息，导致历史依赖操作任务失败；而把过去观测保留在上下文中会带来上下文膨胀与推理延迟。因此需要一种固定开销、可跨策略调用保留情节信息的记忆机制。

Method: MemBodied 由两部分组成：一是关联状态（associative state），记录跨策略调用之间的交互；二是情节锚点（episode anchor），以紧凑表示保存初始场景作为参照。每次策略调用时，动作生成以当前输入 + 两个记忆组件为条件，而非直接拼接历史观测，从而保持固定尺寸记忆与恒定推理成本。

Result: 在 5 个需要记忆能力的 RMBench 任务上，平均成功率分别为无状态策略的 7.81 倍、普通循环记忆的 2.98 倍；相对最强记忆增强基线提升 1.3 倍，且附加参数少 10 倍。在完全可观测的 LIBERO-Long 上达到 90.6%，比无状态 π0 策略提升 5.4%。

Conclusion: 结果表明，固定尺寸情节记忆可作为扩展策略上下文的实用替代方案，在不显著增加参数与推理延迟的前提下有效支撑历史依赖的操作任务。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action models provide a strong foundation for general-purpose robot control, yet a vast majority of policies do not preserve and leverage episode-level information beyond the current observation. This limitation is consequential in history-dependent manipulation tasks that depend on information available only in past observations. Retaining past observations in context can aid in recovering this information, but at the significant cost of ever-growing, bloated context and inference latency. We thus introduce MemBodied, a fixed-size episodic memory with two complementary components: an associative state that records interactions across policy calls and an episode anchor that preserves a compact representation of the initial scene as a reference. At each policy call, the model conditions action generation on the current input and the memory components, rather than directly using past observations. Across five evaluated RMBench tasks requiring memory, MemBodied achieves $7.81\times$ the mean success rate of a stateless policy and $2.98\times$ of vanilla recurrent memory, while outperforming the strongest memory-augmented baseline by $1.3\times$ with $10\times$ fewer added parameters. On the fully observable LIBERO-Long suite, it reached 90.6%, a 5.4% improvement over the stateless $\pi_0$ policy. These findings support MemBodied as a practical alternative to expanding the policy context for history-dependent manipulation.

</details>


### [181] [Generalizable Robotic Insertion with World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28258)
*Nicklas Hansen,Iretiayo Akinola,Yijie Guo,Jie Xu,Bingjie Tang,Hao Su,Xiaolong Wang,Abhishek Gupta,Dieter Fox,Yashraj Narang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于世界模型的通用化机器人插装框架，融合本体感知与腕部相机原始视觉，单一模型在多达90个几何多样任务上训练，对未见物体实现56%零样本成功率，远超无模型基线的7%，并展现数据规模可扩展性与微调数据高效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高混合场景下的机器人装配需要适应多样零件的通用系统，但现有方法依赖针对每个插入任务专门训练的策略，部署新任务耗时费力、流程繁琐，缺乏可扩展性与通用性。

Method: 基于模型的框架：训练单一世界模型，输入机器人本体感知信息与腕部相机采集的原始视觉观测，在多达90个几何多样化的插入任务上联合训练，实现跨任务的通用插装；并可在留出物体上微调。

Result: 对未知几何的未见物体达到56%零样本成功率，无模型基线仅7%；随训练数据中物体数量增加性能提升，体现良好可扩展性；在留出物体上微调相比从头训练显著提升数据效率，部分情形下渐近性能更优。

Conclusion: 这是首个以完全数据驱动方式装配未见物体的系统，向可扩展、通用化的机器人装配系统迈出重要一步。

Abstract: Robotic assembly in high-mixture settings requires adaptable systems that can handle diverse parts, yet current approaches typically rely on policies specialized to each insertion task. Although this can reach high success rates, it makes the process of deploying systems for new problems tedious and time consuming. We present a framework for generalizable insertion using world models that combine robot proprioceptive information with raw visual observations captured by a wrist-mounted camera. Our model-based approach trains a single world model on up to 90 insertion tasks with geometrically diverse parts, achieving 56% zero-shot success on unseen objects with unknown geometry compared to just 7% with a model-free baseline. Importantly, performance improves as more objects are included in the training dataset, demonstrating strong scalability. Lastly, finetuning the generalist model on held-out objects significantly enhances data-efficiency compared to training from scratch and, in some cases, achieves better asymptotic performance. To our knowledge, this is the first system capable of assembling unseen objects in an entirely data-driven manner, and thus represents a significant step toward scalable, generalizable robotic assembly systems.

</details>


### [182] [BrickCraft-Duo: Efficient Dual-Arm Skill Learning and Refinement for Compositional Long-Horizon Assembly](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28281)
*Jichuan Yu,Zhenyu Xiao,Ze Wang,Ruixuan Liu,Changliu Liu,Chuxiong Hu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 BrickCraft-Duo 模块化框架，通过数据高效的技能学习与组合，实现互锁砖块的长时序双臂协同装配，并引入人在回路修正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 互锁砖块装配具有结构多样、步骤依赖复杂、机械交互精细、插入公差紧等特点，是评估真实世界机器人操作能力的代表性平台；而长时序双臂协作装配仍是难题。

Method: 1) 从多样示教中学习可复用的单臂与双臂装配技能；2) 利用双侧对称对齐实现对称臂间技能共享与"装配—支撑"角色分配；3) 基于稳定性感知的装配推理，组合异构技能以完成自主长时序执行；4) 集成人在回路纠错用于针对性技能精修。

Result: 在五项真实世界装配任务（含部分支撑构型、时序步数最多九步）上取得长时序成功率≥60%、单步完成率≥95%。

Conclusion: 模块化技能学习与稳定性感知组合结合人在回路修正，可有效支撑真实双臂长时序互锁装配，验证了数据高效技能复用路线的可行性。

Abstract: Interlocking brick assembly provides a representative testbed for evaluating real-world robotic manipulation capabilities, where diverse structural designs, complex inter-step dependencies, intricate mechanical interactions and tight insertion tolerances pose substantial challenges. We present BrickCraft-Duo, a modular framework for long-horizon dual-arm collaborative assembly of interlocking bricks through data-efficient skill learning and composition. BrickCraft-Duo learns reusable single- and dual-arm assembly skills from diverse demonstrations, with bilateral symmetry alignment facilitating skill sharing across symmetric arms and assembly--support role assignments. Guided by stability-aware assembly reasoning, BrickCraft-Duo composes heterogeneous skills to achieve autonomous long-horizon execution, and further integrates human-in-the-loop correction for targeted skill refinement. The resulting system achieves long-horizon success rates of at least 60% and step-level completion rates of at least 95% across five real-world assembly tasks involving partially supported configurations, with horizons of up to nine steps. Project website: this https URL .

</details>


### [183] [Talk2Escape: Conversational Grounding for Vision-and-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28296)
*Zerui Li,Sihao Lin,Yanyan Shao,Jiwen Zhang,Xiangyu Shi,Shijie Li,Qi Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Talk2Escape，将单轮开环VLN重构为闭环交互过程：轻量视觉语言模块持续监控智能体运动学，检测到局部绕圈或轨迹严重偏离时，把第一视角观测转成简洁的具身查询，向算法oracle或人类索取纠错反馈，从而主动恢复错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLN多为单轮开环范式，感知混叠、传感器噪声与里程计漂移会累积微小偏差，缺乏内在纠错机制，易导致灾难性任务失败。作者希望以主动对话干预实现闭环纠错，且不依赖特定基座智能体。

Method: Talk2Escape是模型无关的主动对话干预框架。核心是轻量VLM模块持续监控智能体运动学；触发条件为检测到局部循环或严重轨迹发散；触发后将原始自我中心观测翻译为简洁、具身grounded的查询，向算法oracle或human-in-the-loop请求针对性纠正反馈，实现闭环导航。

Result: 在R2R-CE、RxR-CE、VLNVerse等高保真仿真器上，对多种基座智能体均取得一致提升；R2R-CE上达到66.0%成功率，超过当前监督式与零样本SOTA。并在Unitree Go2四足机器人上验证了sim-to-real迁移，证明主动对话可显著提升物理环境中的导航鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 通过把导航重新定义为闭环交互过程，Talk2Escape以主动对话干预实现模型无关的错误恢复，在仿真与真实四足平台上均提升鲁棒性与成功率，说明主动对话是增强具身导航可靠性的有效通用机制。

Abstract: While Vision-and-Language Navigation (VLN) has demonstrated remarkable success, the prevailing single-turn paradigm exposes a fundamental vulnerability: agents operate in a strictly open-loop manner. In practice, factors such as perceptual aliasing, sensor noise, and odometry drift can cause minor deviations to accumulate over time, often leading to catastrophic mission failures with no built-in mechanism for error recovery. To address this, we introduce \textit{Talk2Escape}, a proactive and model-agnostic dialogue intervention framework that reframes navigation as a closed-loop interactive process. At its core, a lightweight vision-language module continuously monitors agent kinematics. Upon detecting localized looping or severe trajectory divergence, it translates raw egocentric observations into concise, grounded queries to solicit targeted corrective feedback from either an algorithmic oracle or a human-in-the-loop. Extensive evaluations in high-fidelity simulators, including R2R-CE, RxR-CE, and VLNVerse, demonstrate that \textit{Talk2Escape} exhibits consistent improvements across diverse base agents. Empirically, \textit{Talk2Escape} achieves a 66.0\% Success Rate on R2R-CE, outperforming the current supervised and zero-shot state-of-the-art methods. We further validate its sim-to-real transfer on a Unitree Go2 quadruped, proving that proactive dialogue drastically improves navigation robustness in physical environments.

</details>


### [184] [Contact-Implicit Stein Projected ADMM for Discovery of Diverse Contact-Rich Manipulation Strategies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28299)
*Hrishikesh Sathyanarayan,Christian Hughes,Ian Abraham*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 将Stein变分排斥力施加于Consensus ADMM的分裂变量，使接触隐式轨迹优化由单解坍缩转为输出一组多样的接触丰富策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 接触隐式轨迹优化把接触丰富操作建模为单一约束优化问题，但单次求解只能收敛到众多等价的接触模式、抓取或推动方向中的某一个局部最优，导致策略僵化、对初始化敏感。为使操作更具鲁棒性，需要让求解器能够发现多样的接触策略。

Method: 提出Consensus ADMM的变体并与Stein变分推断结合：在ADMM中引入Stein排斥力，并将其作用于ADMM的分裂变量而非原始变量，从而在可行接触策略集合上实现有效覆盖，同时避免求解器过早停滞。

Result: 在推动、抓取与多机器人交接等多类接触丰富操作任务上验证了方法的有效性；与现有求解器相比，所提求解器形式更简单，并能发现独特的接触模式。

Conclusion: Stein排斥力作用于ADMM分裂变量是获得多样接触策略的有效设计，使接触隐式优化能够输出一组各具差异的可行解，为鲁棒操作提供多样化候选。

Abstract: Contact-implicit trajectory optimization formulates contact-rich manipulation as a single constrained program; however, that single program run collapses onto one local optimum out of many equally valid contact modes, grasps, or push directions. As a consequence, the resulting manipulation strategy is reluctant to change and sensitive to initialization. In order to promote robust manipulation, this paper investigates how contact-implicit solvers can discover diverse contact-rich strategies. Our approach derives a variation of Consensus Alternating Direction Method of Multipliers (ADMM) combined with Stein variational inference methods to output a set of distinct contact-rich solutions. We find that applying the Stein repulsive force to ADMM's split variable (rather than its primal form) allows for effective coverage over the set of feasible contact strategies without prematurely stalling the solver. We demonstrate the effectiveness of our approach on a variety of contact-rich manipulation tasks, including pushing, grasping, and multi-robot handover. Last, we find the proposed solver is simpler in form and capable of discovering unique contact modes when compared with existing solvers. Videos and code with examples are found in this https URL .

</details>


### [185] [VGM-VS: Rethinking Visual Geometry Model for High-Precision Visual Servoing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28312)
*Yimin Pan,Sen Wang,You Zhou,Jianfeng Gao,Pengbo Sun,Ahmed M. Naguib,Zoltan-Csaba Marton*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VGM-VS将预训练前馈视觉几何模型用于闭环PBVS，通过场景级度量适配解决尺度歧义，实现30Hz实时亚毫米级装配精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PBVS依赖精确的相对位姿估计，但在目标被遮挡、弱纹理或占据图像比例很小时传统特征/位姿估计不可靠；而前馈视觉几何模型（VGM）虽有强几何先验，其输出平移存在未知尺度，无法直接用于机器人控制。

Method: 以预训练前馈视觉几何模型从当前视图与目标参考图估计相对相机位姿，并迭代作为闭环PBVS的位姿增量。针对尺度歧义，机器人自主沿预设轨迹从目标位姿出发采集图像-位姿对，微调相机头（camera head）以学习度量尺度，并联合学习手眼变换，从而免去单独标定。

Result: 在三个真实装配任务（USB-C取线、线缆插入、RAM插入）上验证：30Hz实时运行，线缆任务收敛至亚毫米终端精度；目标在伺服过程中移动时成功率90-100%；初始位移达30cm、目标50%遮挡时均可收敛，优于对比的视觉伺服基线。

Conclusion: 前馈视觉几何模型的几何先验可显著提升视觉伺服在遮挡与弱纹理条件下的鲁棒性，而场景级度量微调是将其接入机器人控制的有效桥梁。局限在于每个新场景需自主采集数据并微调。

Abstract: We present VGM-VS, a visual servoing method built on a pretrained feed-forward visual geometry model. Given the current view and a reference image captured at the target configuration, we estimate the relative camera pose with a visual geometry model and apply it iteratively as the pose increment of a closed-loop pose-based visual servoing (PBVS) scheme. The geometry-aware representation acquired from large-scale pretraining keeps this estimate reliable when the target is occluded, weakly textured, or covers only a small part of the image. However, the scale ambiguity inherent to these models leaves the predicted translation defined up to an unknown scale, while the pose increment must be metric for robot control. We close this gap with a scene-specific metric adaptation: the robot autonomously records image--pose pairs along a predefined motion starting from the target pose, and we fine-tune the camera head on these data, jointly learning the hand--eye transform and thus removing the need for a dedicated calibration process. We evaluate our method on three real-world assembly tasks with demanding tolerances: USB-C cable picking, cable insertion, and RAM insertion. Running in real time at 30Hz, VGM-VS converges to submillimeter terminal accuracy on the cable tasks, and reaches success rates of 90--100\% when the target is moved during servoing. It converges in all trials under initial displacements of up to 30cm from the reference pose and with 50\% of the target object occluded, outperforming the compared visual servoing baselines.

</details>


### [186] [TANDEM: Task and Motion Planning with As-Needed Demonstrations for Efficient Vision-Language-Action Model Fine-tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28314)
*Samrat Sahoo,Liang Ji,Tom Silver,Yixuan Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TANDEM 将任务与运动规划（TAMP）与按需人类遥操作结合，用视觉语言模型在线扩展规划域，仅在人无法自动完成的阶段插入"魔法算子"演示，从而高效采集 VLA 微调数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类遥操作大量时间花在机器人已能自主完成的行为上，限制了机器人基础模型数据采集的规模；而固定 TAMP 域无法覆盖长时程操作任务的所有阶段，需要一种能只在必要时求助于人类的组合式数据采集方案。

Method: TANDEM 把人类协助建模为"按需规划能力"：给定语言指令与视觉观测，利用预训练 VLM 为规划域补全缺失谓词并生成由人类执行的 magic operator，使规划器能自主与人工阶段交错执行而无需任务特定干预点；每个阶段后重新感知场景并校验效果是否达成再恢复自主规划；同时用示例预训练轨迹对齐规划器生成的运动与目标 VLA 的预训练数据分布。

Result: 在五个超出 TAMP 域能力的长时程操作任务上，代表性任务中相同人类干预时间下 TANDEM 采集的演示数量是全任务遥操作的 2.9 倍；用每任务 20 条 TANDEM 演示微调预训练 π0.5-DROID 模型，五个任务平均成功率从 0% 提升到 60%。

Conclusion: 将人类遥操作作为可调用的规划算子并辅以 VLM 驱动的规划域扩展与效果校验，能显著提升长时程操作演示的采集效率，并以极少演示量有效微调 VLA 模型。

Abstract: Human teleoperators spend substantial time demonstrating behaviors that robots can already perform autonomously, limiting the scalability of data collection for robot foundation models. Task and motion planning (TAMP) can automate many of these behaviors, but a fixed planning domain may not support every stage of a long-horizon manipulation task. We present TANDEM (Tamp with As-Needed Demonstrations for Efficient Model fine-tuning), a system that combines TAMP with selective human teleoperation to collect demonstrations for tasks beyond the planner's capabilities. Our key idea is to represent human assistance as an on-demand planning capability. Given a language instruction and visual observation, TANDEM uses pretrained vision-language models to extend the planning domain with missing predicates and human-executed magic operators. This allows the planner to interleave autonomous and human-executed stages without task-specific intervention points. After each human stage, TANDEM re-perceives the scene and checks whether the intended effects hold before resuming autonomous planning. To support fine-tuning vision-language-action (VLA) models, TANDEM also uses example pretraining trajectories to align planner-generated motions with the target model's pretraining distribution. We evaluate TANDEM on five long-horizon manipulation tasks beyond the TAMP domain's capabilities. On a representative long-horizon task, TANDEM collects 2.9x as many demonstrations as full-task teleoperation at the same human intervention time. Fine-tuning a pretrained \pi_{0.5}-DROID model on 20 TANDEM demonstrations per task increases average task success from 0% to 60% across the five tasks.

</details>


### [187] [Multimodal Voice Activity Projection for Social Robot Mediation: Expected Behavior and Deployment Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28317)
*Antonio Cano,Guillermo Perez,Luis Merino,Randy Gomez*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 MM-VAP，一种面向机器人调解场景的多模态语音活动预测模型，从同步音视频中预测对话轮次事件，并给出机器人输出接口与部署约束分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交机器人作为人际互动调解者时，期望行为常常是"不说话"，而是定位、等待、避免打断或准备干预，因此需要能感知人类状态并预测对话地板演化的感知层，而非单纯的话轮检测。

Method: 采用音视频预训练 VAP 编码器、LoRA 适配、说话人间注意力机制，基于未来语音活动投影进行零样本事件推断，导出 Hold、Shift、Shift 预测、Backchannel 预测及重叠状态等话轮事件。

Result: 在 NoXi、NoXi+J 和 Haru EDR 数据集上的实验支持该表述的可行性，尤其对可与注视准备、积极倾听和保守干预相关联的地板管理事件表现较好。

Conclusion: MM-VAP 可作为一种人类状态感知层服务于未来机器人调解行为，论文还定义了预期机器人输出接口，并讨论了实时推理、预处理延迟、多模态同步和输入质量监控等部署约束。

Abstract: Turn-taking prediction is especially relevant for social robots that act as mediators in human-human interaction, where the expected action is often not to speak, but to orient, wait, avoid interruption, or prepare a balanced intervention. This paper presents Multimodal Voice Activity Projection (MM-VAP) as a human state-aware perception layer for future robot mediation behavior. The model estimates the future evolution of the conversational floor from synchronized audio-visual evidence and derives turn-taking events such as Hold, Shift, Shift prediction, Backchannel prediction, and overlap-related states. The approach uses VA-related pretrained audio-visual encoders, LoRA adaptation, inter-speaker attention, and zero-shot event inference from future voice activity projections. Experiments on NoXi, NoXi+J, and Haru EDR support the feasibility of this formulation, especially for floor management events that can be connected to gaze preparation, active listening, and conservative intervention. Finally, the paper defines the expected robot output interface and discusses the main deployment constraints, including real-time inference, preprocessing latency, multimodal synchronization, and input-quality monitoring.

</details>


### [188] [Motoneuron-Inspired Sampling for Model Predictive Path Integral Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28325)
*Alexis Poignant,Jan Babič*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 Spike-MPPI：用简化的运动神经元动力学生成时间结构化的采样扰动，在 MuJoCo Ant 上与高斯采样及谱匹配高斯对比，发现结构化采样显著提升控制平滑度，任务表现依赖 rollout 条件与驱动方式，谱匹配能复现部分效应但完整 Spike 方案还存在高阶统计组织的额外作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MPPI 依赖随机轨迹采样，在有限 rollout 预算下性能强烈依赖提议分布结构。标准做法用高斯噪声扰动控制序列，但已有证据表明时间相关、结构化采样能改善有限预算下的控制。作者想借鉴生物运动神经元的脉冲发放特性，设计结构化提议分布。

Method: 提出 motoneuron 启发的 Spike 提议分布，通过简化的运动神经元动力学模型生成时间结构化扰动；在统一 MPPI 框架下，于力矩驱动与拮抗驱动的 MuJoCo Ant 模型上评测，对比标准高斯采样与谱匹配高斯（spectrum-matched Gaussian）控制，以分离二阶频谱结构与更高阶统计组织的作用。

Result: 结构化采样显著提升执行控制平滑度（executed-control smoothness）；对任务性能的影响取决于 rollout 条件与机器人驱动方式。谱匹配高斯能复现相当一部分观察到的行为，但完整 Spike 提议仍保留超出二阶频谱结构的额外效应。

Conclusion: 提议分布设计应被视为二阶频谱结构与更高阶统计组织的结合；运动神经元启发的结构化采样是改善有限预算 MPPI 的有前景方向。

Abstract: Model Predictive Path Integral (MPPI) control relies on stochastic trajectory sampling, and its performance under limited rollout budgets depends strongly on the structure of the proposal distribution. Standard implementations commonly perturb control sequences with Gaussian noise, despite growing evidence that temporally correlated and structured sampling can improve finite-budget control. We introduce Spike-MPPI, a motoneuron-inspired proposal that generates temporally structured perturbations through a simplified model of motoneuron dynamics. The proposal is evaluated within a common MPPI framework on torque-actuated and antagonistically actuated MuJoCo Ant models against standard Gaussian sampling and spectrum-matched Gaussian controls. Results show that structured sampling substantially improves executed-control smoothness, while its effect on task performance depends on rollout condition and robot actuation. Spectrum matching reproduces a substantial part of the observed behavior, while the full Spike proposal retains additional effects beyond second-order spectral structure. These results support treating proposal design as a combination of second-order spectral structure and higher-order statistical organization.

</details>


### [189] [Beyond Future Prediction: Denoising as Generative Adaptation for Robot Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28339)
*Zanyi Wang,Yuheng Lei,Dengyang Jiang,Ping Luo,Mengdi Wang,Zhixuan Liang,Shilong Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 NowWAM：无需未来视觉预测目标，直接在预训练生成式 DiT 的去噪轨迹上共同训练，同时去噪当前观测并预测动作。在 LIBERO-Plus 上达 87.7%（FLUX2-Klein），比未来目标共训基线高 6.1 分，视觉 token 减半，步时提速 1.8 倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人策略普遍通过"预测未来视觉"来迁移预训练生成式 DiT 的视觉先验，但该迁移方式是否必要、预训练生成先验究竟为动作学习贡献了什么并不清楚。作者希望回答这一基础问题，并给出更合理的控制适配方式。

Method: NowWAM 是一种"无未来目标"的共训范式：在同一个视觉流上对当前观测执行去噪，并同步预测机器人动作，使原生生成目标与面向动作的表征沿整条去噪轨迹直接耦合。作者在受控设置下对比了"过去/未来视觉目标"与"仅用干净端点"等变体。

Result: 受控实验表明：过去与未来视觉目标表现相当，而仅训练干净端点会显著降低鲁棒性，说明独立未来目标并非必需，去噪轨迹才是有效的控制接口。LIBERO-Plus 上 NowWAM 达 87.7%（FLUX2-Klein），较未来目标共训基线高 6.1 分，视觉 token 由 784 降至 392，步时由 2.85 s 降至 1.63 s（1.8 倍加速）；使用纯文生图主干 Z-Image 亦达 87.8%。

Conclusion: 生成式 DiT 先验对控制的价值不在于显式未来视觉预测，而在于沿去噪轨迹构建面向动作的表征；该适配方式与视频生成或图像编辑主干无强绑定，纯文生图主干同样有效。

Abstract: Pretrained generative Diffusion Transformers (DiTs) capture rich pixel-level visual and language-conditioned structure through large-scale image and video generation training. A growing line of robot policies builds on this generative prior, but how it should be transferred to control remains unclear, and existing approaches commonly instantiate this transfer through future visual prediction. We ask a more basic question: what a pretrained generative DiT actually contributes to action learning, and how this prior should be adapted for control. We introduce NowWAM, a future-target-free co-training formulation that denoises the current observation and predicts robot actions from the same visual stream, directly coupling the native generative objective to the action-facing representation across the denoising trajectory. Under matched controlled settings, past and future visual targets perform comparably, while restricting training to the clean endpoint substantially reduces robustness, suggesting that a separate future target is not essential for generative adaptation, while the denoising trajectory remains an effective interface for control. On LIBERO-Plus, NowWAM reaches 87.7% with FLUX2-Klein, improving over the future-target co-training baseline by 6.1 points while halving training visual tokens (784 to 392) and reducing step time from 2.85 s to 1.63 s, a 1.8x speedup. With the pure text-to-image Z-Image backbone, NowWAM further reaches 87.8%, showing that strong control adaptation is not tied to video generation or image-editing backbones.

</details>


### [190] [LEAP-CBF: A Safety Filter for Uncertain Systems with Least-Effort Adversarial Potentials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28364)
*Oswin So,Eric Yu,Chuchu Fan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Least-Effort Adversarial Potentials (LEAP)证书，量化状态对抗扰动的鲁棒性，证明其为无扰动系统的CBF，可用于构造对累积努力有界扰动鲁棒的安全滤波器，并用on-policy深度强化学习构造，在多智能体仿真及四足、四旋翼真机上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 鲁棒CBF构造困难且对高维、输入受限系统过于保守，需要一种更易构造、保守性更低的安全鲁棒性刻画方式。

Method: 引入LEAP（以扰动导致失效所需"努力"量化状态鲁棒性）；证明其作为无扰动系统的控制屏障函数；据此设计对累积努力有界扰动鲁棒的安全滤波器；采用on-policy深度强化学习构造LEAP。

Result: 在多类带扰动与不确定性的多智能体系统仿真中验证，并在四足机器人与四旋翼真机实验中证明LEAP适用于真实机器人系统的扰动与不确定性。

Conclusion: LEAP提供了构造简便、保守性较低的鲁棒安全证书，兼具理论保证与真机可行性，可用于真实机器人安全滤波。

Abstract: Control barrier functions (CBF) are a popular safety filter to ensure safety for nonlinear dynamical systems. However, when the system is subject to uncertainties and disturbances, this requires the use of robust variants of CBFs, which can be difficult to construct and can be overly conservative, especially for high-dimensional systems under input constraints. In this work, we propose a new approach to solve these challenges by introducing Least-Effort Adversarial Potentials (LEAP), a certificate that quantifies the robustness of a given state against disturbances in terms of the effort required by the disturbance to cause failure. We show that LEAP is a CBF for the undisturbed system, but can also be used to construct a safety filter that is robust to disturbances whose cumulative effort is bounded. We propose a method for constructing LEAPs with on-policy deep reinforcement learning. Next, we demonstrate LEAPs in simulation on a variety of multi-agent systems with disturbances and uncertainties. Finally, hardware experiments on a quadruped and quadrotors validate that LEAPs are well suited to tackle the disturbances and uncertainties from real-world robotic systems.

</details>


### [191] [Amplify: A Lightweight Library for Reproducible Nonlinear Programming Problems in Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28377)
*Nelson Rosa Jr*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出轻量级非线性规划库 Amplify（537行），以声明式编程在优化模型内直接表达轨迹优化算法，面向机器人问题的可复现性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人领域优化问题的复现性差：算法实现、求解器配置与建模语言分离，导致结果难以复现；同时现有转录（transcription）库依赖重、门槛高。

Method: 仅依赖网络连接、AMPL 建模语言与文本编辑器；将动力学计算、轨迹生成与参考运动算法作为 OP 的约束/目标/决策变量，在声明式范式中直接嵌入优化模型；并给出该形式化在其它轻量可复现转录库中的实现准则。

Result: 与另外 3 个库在跨领域基准优化问题上对比，涵盖双足运动与抓取规划等例子。

Conclusion: 声明式、模型内算法表达可在极简代码量下实现轨迹优化并提升可复现性，为轻量可复现的转录库设计提供可迁移范式。

Abstract: Optimization problems (OPs) are key to solving many challenging research problems in robotics. However, reproducibility still remains a major issue. In this paper, we present Amplify, a lightweight nonlinear programming library aimed at reproducible results of robotic-related trajectory optimization problems. The minimalistic requirements for the 537-line library (80 characters per line) are an Internet connection, familiarity with the AMPL modeling language, and a text editor. Our primary contribution is the formulation of a library where trajectory optimization algorithms are represented directly within the optimization model. Specifically, we implement the algorithms used to compute the dynamics, trajectories, and reference motions as constraints of the OP in a declarative programming paradigm. We outline how our formulation of objectives, decisions variables, and constraints can be implemented in other transcription libraries that want to be lightweight and reproducible. We also compare the Amplify framework with 3 other libraries across examples of benchmark optimization problems across several fields, including bipedal locomotion and grasp planning.

</details>


### [192] [ForgetMimic: Motion Unlearning for Reinforcement Learning Humanoid Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28378)
*Xukun Luan,Zhongxiang Lei,Chen Gong,Shaowei Li,Yuanguo Bi,Jinyan Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ForgetMimic 是首个面向物理世界人形机器人控制的运动级"遗忘"方法：给定在 N 个动作上训练的策略 π_θ，在退化目标 K 个动作的同时保持其余 N-K 个动作性能，并识别出导致遗忘失败的两个关键训练机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于人类演示的强化学习使人形机器人具备多样、敏捷、自然的运动能力，但如何从已学策略中消除特定动作尚缺乏探索。其动机来自安全与隐私需求：移除恶意、被投毒或次优动作，以及受版权保护、需满足 GDPR 等"被遗忘权"的运动。

Method: 提出运动级机器遗忘方法 ForgetMimic：以在 N 个运动上训练的策略为输入，对目标子集 K 个运动进行性能退化（记忆消除），同时保持其余 N-K 个运动有效；并定位与解决机器人控制中引起遗忘失败的两个关键训练机制。

Result: 在 Unitree G1 与 H2 人形机器人、共 12 个运动（含 Dance、Fight、Flip 等）上开展大量实验，结果表明 ForgetMimic 能有效消除指定运动的记忆，同时保持其他所有运动正常运行。

Conclusion: 该工作首次将机器遗忘引入物理世界人形机器人运动控制，实现运动级选择性遗忘，兼顾安全性、隐私合规与其余技能性能保持。

Abstract: Humanoid control, leveraging human demonstrations, has achieved diverse, agile, and natural locomotion behaviors through reinforcement learning (RL). While this paradigm has yielded remarkable performance in physical humanoid control, how to eliminate specific motions from learned policies remains insufficiently explored. Addressing this issue is motivated by pressing safety and privacy concerns: the removal of malicious, poisoned, or suboptimal motions, as well as copyright-protected motions subject to the right to be forgotten under regulations such as the GDPR, is of critical importance. To this end, we propose {ForgetMimic}, the first motion-level unlearning method designed specifically for physical-world humanoid control. The core idea of ForgetMimic is as follows: given a policy $\pi_\theta$ trained on $N$ motions, our method degrades performance on a target subset of $K$ motions while preserving the effectiveness of the remaining $N-K$ motions. Furthermore, we identify and resolve two key training mechanisms in robot control that lead to unlearning failure. We conduct extensive experiments on the Unitree G1 and H2 humanoid robots across 12 motions, including Dance, Fight, Flip, and others. Experimental results demonstrate that ForgetMimic effectively eliminates memory of designated motions while maintaining the normal operation of all other motions.

</details>


### [193] [PointCast: One World Model for Rigid, Articulated, and Deformable Object Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28393)
*Hantao Ye,Ross Worobel,Zhuoli Xie,Mingen Li,Houjian Yu,Youngjin Hong,Changhyun Choi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PointCast：以点集为状态、DiT为骨干的机器人操作世界模型，单一架构覆盖刚体、布料、绳索与多关节物体，并用于MPC规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人操作需要在执行动作前预测动作如何改变物体状态，因此需要世界模型；但现有方法多受限于网格/拓扑先验或单一物体类型（刚体、关节体、可变形体各自建模），难以用统一、无网格且拓扑无关的表示覆盖多种操作形态。

Method: 状态表示为物体与末端执行器上的一组3D点，无网格、拓扑无关；每点保持自身身份并按各自轨迹监督（point-wise trajectory supervision），从而学习"每个点去哪"而非仅点集形状。骨干为扩散Transformer，去噪未来短时窗的点位置，条件为点的近期历史与末端执行器指令运动；注意力交替进行局部与全局建模，并通过到末端执行器的交叉注意力承载耦合。19.8M参数，四种形态（刚体、布料、绳索、多关节柜）各训练一个checkpoint，共享同一架构与训练配方。

Result: 在随机化仿真中与四个基线在同一指标上对比，四类形态中三类最优、刚体第二；在真实机器人遥操作数据集上，六类中四类平均误差最低、其余两类第二，并在全部六类上优于该数据集自带模型；零样本条件下仿真checkpoint在四类拍摄场景中两类最优。冻结后嵌入基于采样的MPC（每个窗口一次网络评估），在64个episode的四项仿真任务中规划性能不劣于或在每项任务上超过所有基线。

Conclusion: 点集+扩散Transformer的无网格世界模型可作为跨刚体、可变形与关节体的统一预测器，且冻结后可直接用于规划；点身份监督与局部/全局交替注意是统一建模的关键，但不同形态仍需分别训练checkpoint。

Abstract: World models are useful for robotic manipulation because robots can predict how actions change the states of objects before executing them. We present PointCast, a point-set world model that spans rigid, articulated, and deformable object manipulation. Its state is a set of 3D points on the object and the end-effector, mesh-free and topology-agnostic. Each point keeps its identity and is supervised on its own trajectory, which teaches the model where every point goes rather than only the shape the points form. Its backbone is a diffusion transformer that denoises a short window of future point positions, conditioned on the points' recent history and the commanded end-effector motion. The backbone's attention alternates between local and global, and cross-attention to the end-effector carries the coupling. This one architecture at 19.8M parameters and one training recipe cover four regimes, rigid objects, cloth, rope, and multi-joint cabinets, with a separate checkpoint trained for each. Trained on randomized simulation and scored against four baselines on the same metric, it is best on three of four regimes and second on rigid. Trained on a real-world robot teleoperation dataset, it has the lowest mean error in four of its six categories, is second in the other two, and improves on the dataset's own model in all six; zero-shot, its simulation checkpoints are best on two of four captures. Frozen inside sampling-based model-predictive control at one network evaluation per window, it plans four simulated tasks over 64 episodes, competitive with or outperforming every baseline on each. Project website at this https URL .

</details>


### [194] [Tractable Reinforcement Learning for Full Class of Signal Temporal Logic Specifications Using Spatiotemporal Tube Reward](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28396)
*Vaishnavi Jagabathula,P Sangeerth,Pushpak Jagtap*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 将STL高层规格映射为时空管(STT)的时变几何边界，用时柔Actor-Critic在增广时间维上直接约束状态，无需状态历史即可满足输入约束与规格鲁棒满足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非完整/欠驱动机器人在未知动力学与严格执行器限幅下需满足复杂时序逻辑规格。传统STT解析控制器难以强制输入约束，现有RL方法依赖记忆密集的状态历史，二者均受限。

Method: 以STL描述高层规格，利用时空管(STT)几何特性将逻辑与时序复杂度映射为时变几何边界；对多维系统状态直接施加约束而非标量鲁棒度；状态空间增广时间维，用连续、几何感知的奖励训练时柔SAC智能体，避免执行时显式评估复杂逻辑语义。

Result: 摘要仅声明框架具备历史无关、计算高效特性，能学习在严格遵守输入约束下鲁棒满足规格的连续控制策略，未给出具体量化实验结果。

Conclusion: 该框架在原理上同时规避了STT控制器难处理输入约束与RL依赖状态历史两方面的局限，为STL约束下的机器人学习控制提供了一种免历史、几何驱动的新范式。

Abstract: This paper addresses the control problem for robotic systems, including non-holonomic and underactuated platforms operating under unknown dynamics and strict actuator limits to satisfy complex high-level specifications. We denote these high-level specifications using Signal Temporal Logic (STL) and propose a novel time-aware Reinforcement Learning (RL) framework that leverages the geometric properties of Spatiotemporal Tubes (STTs). While traditional analytical STT controllers often struggle to enforce input constraints, and existing RL approaches rely on memory-intensive state history, our method natively overcomes both limitations. By mapping the logical and temporal complexities of the full class of STL into time-varying geometric boundaries, we directly constrain the multidimensional system state without relying on scalar robustness metrics. Augmenting the state space with time, we train a time-aware Soft Actor-Critic (SAC) agent using a continuous, geometry-aware reward function that eliminates the need to explicitly evaluate complex logical semantics during execution. The proposed framework offers a history-free, computationally efficient approach to learn continuous control policies that ensure robust satisfaction of specifications while strictly adhering to system input constraints.

</details>


### [195] [Watch, Recall, Act: Always-On Robots in Concurrent Embodied Streams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28429)
*Ding Yi,Peiwen Sun,Chenchu Rong,Jianan Wang,Xili Dai,Xiangyu Yue,Xi Lin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ARMS 是一个面向"永续在线"机器人流式任务的策略框架：以单预训练 π0.5 为骨架，外挂三个轻量模块异步提供实时感知、本体状态与自身历史动作上下文，实现双臂并发执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动作模型假设固定指令、无中途干预、单步推理，而真实机器人处于永不重置的连续流中：指令随时到达与失效、场景持续变化、自身过去动作改变当前推理需求。机器人需同时前瞻远期线索、回看远期自身动作、并在双臂并发下行动。

Method: 提出 ARMS：单一预训练 π0.5 骨架 + 三个轻量模块，将实时感知、embodied states 与自身历史动作转成上下文供骨架读取后再行动；模块异步更新上下文，使"看"和"回忆"不阻塞"行动"，双臂同时执行。引入 agent-causal self-history 记录哪只手臂何时做了什么。为免额外标注，构建 ARMS Dataset，其分阶段构建脚本本身从真实双臂遥操作数据中为每个模块自动打标。

Result: 在组合任务上达到 45%，而四个主要基线中最强者为 28%；消融实验证实记忆模块、本体状态头与异步并发机制各自均为必要。

Conclusion: ARMS 表明以极简的"上下文提供器 + 骨架策略"集成方式即可支撑永续在线、双臂并发的机器人流式任务，且可通过构建脚本自动监督各模块而无需额外标注。

Abstract: An always-on robot faces an endless stream that never resets: instructions arrive and lapse, the scene changes, and its own past actions reshape what it must reason about. Today's action models are built for the opposite: a fixed instruction, no mid-task intervention, single-step reasoning. In an open-ended world a robot must watch a live stream for far-future cues, recall its own far-past actions, and act on them under dual-arm concurrency. We present ARMS (Always-on Robot in Multi-modal Streams), a deliberately simple streaming policy: a single pretrained $\pi$0.5 backbone augmented by three lightweight modules that turn live perception, embodied states, and the robot's own past actions into context the backbone reads before it acts. The modules update this context asynchronously, so watching and recalling never block acting and the two arms act at once. Rather than inventing new mechanisms, ARMS integrates these learned context providers with an agent-causal self-history that logs which arm did what, and when. To supervise them without extra annotation, we build ARMS Dataset, whose staged construction script itself labels every module from real dual-arm teleoperation. Trained on it, ARMS reaches 45% on the combined task against 28% for the strongest of our four main baselines, and ablations confirm the memory module, the embodied-state head, and asynchronous concurrency are each necessary.

</details>


### [196] [LiMA: Bridging Long-term Imagination to Real-time Dexterous Manipulation via Asynchronous Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28431)
*Ning Chen,Yankai Fu,Junkai Zhao,Qianpu Sun,Guocai Yao,Pengwei Wang,Zhongyuan Wang,Shanghang Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 LiMA，一种异步双系统生成式框架，通过慢系统生成稀疏长时程时空意图、快系统做稠密高频动作精修，并用 Latent Schrödinger Bridge Coupling 对齐两者，降低推理延迟并在双臂灵巧操作任务上取得较好成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灵巧操作既需长时程前瞻又需快速反应控制。VLA 模型高层推理强但缺乏细粒度物理动力学与空间感知；WAM 则因迭代生成导致推理延迟高。二者共同造成时间错配——模型意图无法适应快速变化的物理接触。作者希望从架构上解耦意图规划与反应执行以突破该瓶颈。

Method: 提出 LiMA 异步双系统生成框架。计算按多尺度分层：慢系统负责稀疏长时程时空意图生成，快系统负责稠密高频运动精修。为对齐稀疏意图与稠密动作轨迹，引入 Latent Schrödinger Bridge Coupling 机制，将精修建模为熵正则化的概率传输过程。通过异步解耦实现低延迟推理。

Result: 相比 Cosmos-Policy，推理延迟降低 45.8%。在覆盖多时程的六个双臂灵巧操作任务上，整体成功率达 70.8%，平均子任务成功率 78.9%，并在未见场景中保持性能。

Conclusion: 异步解耦的慢-快双系统设计可有效缓解 VLA/WAM 的时间错配问题，在灵巧操作中兼顾长时程意图与高频反应，同时显著降低推理延迟；跨任务与未见场景的泛化性初步得到验证。

Abstract: Dexterous manipulation demands long-term foresight and rapid reactive control. Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models, while proficient in high-level reasoning, often lack a fine-grained understanding of physical dynamics and spatial perception. Conversely, World-Action Models (WAMs) typically suffer from high inference latency due to iterative generation. These deficiencies result in a critical temporal misalignment where the model's intent fails to adapt to rapid physical contact changes. To overcome this fundamental bottleneck, we propose LiMA, an asynchronous dual-system generative framework that systematically decouples intent planning from reactive execution. LiMA organizes computation into a multi-scale hierarchy: a slow system handles sparse long-horizon spatiotemporal intent generation, while a fast system focuses on dense high-frequency motion refinement. To align sparse intent predictions with dense action trajectories, we introduce a Latent Schrödinger Bridge Coupling mechanism that formulates refinement as an entropy-regularized probabilistic transport process. LiMA reduces inference latency by 45.8% compared with Cosmos-Policy via asynchronous decoupling. Evaluated across six bimanual dexterous manipulation tasks spanning multiple horizons, LiMA achieves an overall success rate of 70.8% and an average subtask success rate of 78.9%, while maintaining performance in unseen scenarios. The project website is available at this https URL

</details>


### [197] [Where Should I Join? Robot Group Joining via Language-Guided Goal Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28467)
*Zilin Fang,Zishuo Wang,Gim Hee Lee,David Hsu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出“语言引导的机器人群体加入”任务：依据自然语言描述定位目标群体并预测合社交规范的加入位姿，用谱聚类生成候选子集、语言条件图像-几何模型排序，再用人类队形先验生成多模态能量-朝向图预测位姿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交导航通常假设目标已给定，而机器人加入群体需根据群体实时活动与队形推断加入位置，属高语义任务，对导盲机器人、自主代步车等应用有价值。

Method: 递归谱划分生成结构化候选子集，用语言条件图像-几何模型排序实现语言接地；基于接地群体，用人类队形先验的目标预测器在可行位姿上生成多模态能量-朝向图。

Result: 在对话、排队、观众三类场景及不同群体规模、人群密度、视觉歧义下，接地精度具竞争力、推理亚秒级，加入位姿预测优于所有基线；真机实验展示静态与动态交互中的群体加入。

Conclusion: 语言引导的群体加入可被分解为群体接地与位姿预测两步，且方法在仿真评测与真机场景中均可行，为社交导航提供新任务与基线。

Abstract: Social navigation typically assumes a specified goal and focuses on reaching it while respecting social conventions, whereas robot group joining requires predicting where to join based on the group's real-time activity and formation. This is a highly semantic task, yet an important capability for applications such as robotic guide dogs and autonomous mobility scooters. We formulate language-grounded robot group joining: given an observation and a natural-language description of a target group, the robot identifies the relevant group members and predicts socially compliant joining poses. For grounding, we generate structured candidate subsets through recursive spectral partitioning and rank them with a language-conditioned image--geometry model. Given the grounded group, a goal predictor leverages human-formation priors to produce a multimodal energy--orientation map over feasible robot poses. Experiments on conversations, queues, and audiences across varying group sizes, crowd densities, and visual ambiguities show that our method achieves competitive grounding accuracy with sub-second inference and outperforms all baselines in joining-pose prediction. Real-robot experiments further demonstrate group joining in both static and dynamically changing interactions.

</details>
